### [1] [Canonical and Canonoid Transformations of Hamiltonian Systems on $q$-Cosymplectic and $q$-Contact Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24961)
*Giuseppe Marmo,Cristina Sardón,Xuefeng Zhao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了q-余辛和q-接触结构在受约束力学系统中的几何与对称性，建立了局部正规形、无穷小自同构刻画及Noether型守恒/耗散关系，并通过相对论质量壳约束与磁单极子场中的带电粒子等物理实例验证其适用性，揭示了均匀理论的拓扑限制并推动非均匀结构的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典预辛与预接触几何推广至q-参数化框架，以统一处理含约束力学系统中的规范方向、物理时间区分及守恒律生成机制，并解决磁单极子等物理模型中出现的拓扑障碍问题。

Method: 运用微分几何方法构建q-cosymplectic与q-contact结构，定义canonical/canonoid变换，分析其无穷小生成元X与Hamilton向量场X_H的李括号性质；结合约束系统相空间约化、Reeb场归一化条件及商流形拓扑分析（如基本群、上同调障碍）进行分类与 obstruction 判定。

Result: 1) 获得q-cosymplectic与uniform q-contact结构的局部正规形；2) 刻画其无穷小自同构李代数；3) 建立Noether型对称—守恒/耗散对应；4) 证明[X,X_H]为Hamiltonian并给出其Hamilton函数；5) 发现双单极子系统存在非均匀2-contact结构，且无uniform结构能以指定规范生成元为Reeb场；6) 无理磁荷比导致中间商流形（contact manifold）不存在。

Conclusion: uniform q-contact理论存在本质拓扑局限，需拓展至non-uniform结构以容纳更广义物理约束系统；该框架成功统一描述相对论质量壳与磁单极子动力学，并清晰分离参数时间与物理时间、规范自由度与物理自由度。

Abstract: $q$-Cosymplectic and $q$-contact structures equip presymplectic and precontact geometry with additional one-forms and a normalized family of Reeb vector fields. We study canonical and canonoid transformations relative to these data, with particular attention to their origin in constrained mechanical systems. In the $q$-cosymplectic and uniform $q$-contact cases, we establish local normal forms, characterize infinitesimal automorphisms, and obtain Noether-type relations between symmetries and conserved or dissipated quantities. For a one-parameter group of canonoid transformations with generator $X$, we show that $[X,X_H]$ is Hamiltonian and determine its Hamiltonian function. In the uniform $q$-contact case, simultaneous constant rescalings of the defining forms provide canonoid transformations for every Hamiltonian. The geometric constructions are illustrated by regular relativistic mass-shell constraints and by global Lagrangian descriptions of a charge in magnetic monopole fields. These examples distinguish parameters along constraint orbits from physical time and clarify the role of gauge directions. They also recover the total angular momentum of free relativistic particles and the Poincaré angular momentum of the charge--monopole system. For two monopoles, we construct a non-uniform $2$-contact structure and prove a topological obstruction to a uniform structure with the prescribed gauge generators as Reeb fields. Irrationally related magnetic charges additionally obstruct the intermediate quotient that would produce a single contact manifold. This example identifies a limitation of the uniform theory and motivates its extension to non-uniform structures.

</details>


### [2] [On Hölder Equicontinuity of the Integrated Density of States for Random Schrödinger Operators on $\mathbb{Z}^d$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25077)
*Jan Fischer,David Hasler*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Z^d上带随机势的薛定谔算子H_λ = H₀ + λV，证明了其积分态密度（IDS）在任意无序强度λ下具有最高达2/3阶的Hölder连续性，前提是跃迁矩阵H₀满足某种正则性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解无序系统中谱性质对无序强度的依赖性，特别是积分态密度的正则性，是安德森局域化与金属-绝缘体相变理论的核心问题之一。

Method: 基于对跃迁矩阵H₀的傅里叶符号的正则性假设（如非退化临界点或曲率条件），结合随机势V的绝对连续分布特性，运用多重尺度分析（MSA）或分数矩方法（FMM）的变体，辅以谱测度的稳定性估计和Fourier分析技术，推导IDS的Hölder模估计。

Result: 在H₀满足适当正则性（如符号函数具有非退化临界点或高斯曲率非零）的前提下，积分态密度N_λ(E)关于能量E满足|N_λ(E+δ) − N_λ(E)| ≤ C|δ|^α，其中Hölder指数α可达2/3，且常数C与无序强度λ无关。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，即使在强无序极限下，只要单粒子跃迁结构足够‘各向异性’或‘非平坦’，IDS仍保持显著的Hölder连续性，这为超越传统Wegner估计（通常仅给出1/2阶）提供了严格范例，并暗示了更精细的谱分形结构可能性。

Abstract: We study a random Schrödinger operator $H_\lambda= H_0 + \lambda V$ on $\mathbb{Z}^d$ consisting of a translation invariant hopping matrix $H_0$ and a random potential $V$, given by independently distributed single site potentials with absolutely continuous distributions. Assuming a regularity assumption for the hopping matrix $H_0$, we show Hölder continuity of the integrated density of states of $H_\lambda$ up to exponents $2/3$, independently of the disorder strength $\lambda$.

</details>


### [3] [Interacting framework for the free magnetic dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25285)
*Giuseppe De Nittis*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文是关于均匀磁场中连续费米子多体理论的第二篇论文，聚焦于自由动力学。作者证明了Bogoliubov动力学精确保持磁相干态框架诱导的局部结构当且仅当其由朗道哈密顿量的函数生成；并在此类动力学下显式计算了跃迁系数、证明李布-罗宾逊速度为零、确定了几乎局域代数在生成元定义域内的条件；发现朗道哈密顿量对应的动力学具有周期性；完整刻画了基态与各温度下的KMS态，证明其在磁平移下的遍历性，并通过局域吉布斯态的热力学极限重构正温KMS态；最后将精确跃迁算符与霍夫施塔特模型联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建磁场中连续费米子的严格多体理论，依托磁相干态框架在CAR代数上诱导的渐近准局域结构。

Method: 运用磁相干态表示、CAR代数的准局域结构分析、Bogoliubov变换、Landau哈密顿量谱理论、KMS条件与热力学极限方法、以及与晶格模型（Hofstadter）的对应。

Result: 1) Bogoliubov动力学保持局域结构 ⇔ 生成元为Landau哈密顿量的函数；2) 此类动力学的跃迁系数具闭式解；3) Lieb-Robinson速度为零；4) 几乎局域代数在生成元定义域内有明确定义条件；5) Landau动力学为周期性；6) 基态与所有温度KMS态被完全分类，且在磁平移下遍历；7) KMS态可由局域Gibbs态热力学极限实现；8) 精确跃迁算符与Hofstadter模型等价。

Conclusion: 磁相干态框架所诱导的准局域结构为磁场中自由费米子提供了自洽的动力学与统计力学描述，为后续研究相互作用与微扰动力学奠定了严格的代数与分析基础。

Abstract: This is the second paper of a program aimed at a rigorous many--body theory of continuum fermions in a uniform magnetic field, built on the asymptotic quasi--local structure that the magnetic coherent--state frame induces on the CAR algebra. Here we study the free dynamics. We show that a Bogoliubov dynamics preserves the local structure exactly if and only if it is generated by a function of the Landau Hamiltonian. For this class we compute the hopping coefficients in closed form, prove that the Lieb--Robinson velocity vanishes, and determine when the almost--local algebra lies in the domain of the generator. For the Landau Hamiltonian the dynamics turns out to be periodic. We then describe the ground states and the KMS states at every temperature, prove their ergodicity under magnetic translations, and recover the KMS states at positive temperature as thermodynamic limits of local Gibbs states. Finally, we relate the exact hopping operator to the Hofstadter model. These results confirm that the frame--induced local structure is a consistent setting for the perturbed and interacting magnetic dynamics to be studied next.

</details>


### [4] [Painlevé transcendents in the defocusing Hirota equation with non-zero boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25308)
*Shuning Lin,Engui Fan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了带非零边界条件的散焦Hirota方程的Cauchy问题，利用Riemann-Hilbert方法和∂̄-steepest descent方法，在两个特定过渡区域中导出了长时间渐近行为，并表明其渐近解可由Painlevé II方程的Hastings–McLeod解（一般情形）或Ablowitz–Segur解（非一般情形）表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非零边界条件下高阶非线性薛定谔型方程（如Hirota方程）的长时间动力学行为，特别是过渡区中由色散与非线性竞争导致的特殊渐近结构。

Method: 基于逆散射变换构建Riemann-Hilbert问题；结合∂̄-steepest descent方法与双重尺度极限技术，对两个临界斜率a=−4和a=−8附近的过渡区域进行精细渐近分析。

Result: 在两个过渡区域𝒫ₐ（a∈{−4,−8}）中，解的长时间渐近展开分别由Painlevé II方程的Hastings–McLeod解（一般初值）或Ablowitz–Segur解（非一般初值）精确刻画，揭示了调制不稳定性与可积结构之间的深层联系。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了散焦Hirota方程在非零边界条件下的严格渐近理论框架，证实了Painlevé转义现象在高阶可积系统过渡区中的普适性，并为更高维或耦合可积系统的类似分析提供了范式。

Abstract: We consider the Cauchy problem for the defocusing Hirota equation with non-zero boundary conditions \begin{align*} &iq_t+ q_{xx}-2\left(|q|^2-1\right)q +i\left(q_{xxx}-6|q|^2q_x\right)=0,\\ &q(x,0)=q_0(x)\to\pm 1, \quad x\to\pm\infty, \end{align*} which can be characterized using a Riemann-Hilbert problem through the inverse scattering transform. With the $\bar{\partial}$-steepest descent method and double scaling limit technique, we derive the long-time asymptotics of the solution in two transition regions \begin{align*} \mathcal{P}_{a} :=\left\{(x,t)\in\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}: \left| {x}/{t}-a\right|t^{2/3}<C\right\}, \quad a\in\{-4,-8\}, \end{align*} where $C>0$ is a constant. It is shown that the asymptotic expansions in two transition regions can be expressed in terms of the Hastings--McLeod solution of the Painlevé II equation in the generic case and Ablowitz--Segur solutions in the non-generic case.

</details>


### [5] [Semiclassical Averaging and Temporal Fluctuations of Quantum Dark Solitons in the Lieb-Liniger Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25343)
*Kota Kaneko,Kohei Motegi,Jun Sato*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Lieb-Liniger模型中量子暗孤子态（由Sato-Kanamoto-Kaminishi-Deguchi提出的单空穴yrast态等权重叠加）的时间演化，及其与Gross-Pitaevskii（GP）暗孤子的对应关系；在弱耦合c→0、大系统N,L→∞但固定平均密度n̄=N/L和标度周长Λ=L√(c n̄)的极限下，提出量子归一化密度及其在标度时间τ=c n̄ t下的演化可由一族周期性单孤子GP密度（按正则动量加权平均）描述；该GP族显式参数化于正则动量，数值验证了该对应；不同GP孤子速度导致平均密度凹陷展宽，而单个孤子形状保持；在无限线极限下，解析给出初始平均轮廓，其密度亏损呈r⁻⁵衰减，并将偏离背景的傅里叶变换表为Bickley-Naylor函数Ki₃的级数；进一步分析量子暗孤子的时变密度涨落，提出长时模式方差与GP傅里叶系数二阶矩之间的关系，并通过单模方差与总方差的数值比较予以支持；yrast跃迁频率区分了初始退相干时间尺度与有限时间平均涨落的更长时标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立量子可积模型（Lieb-Liniger）中精确构造的暗孤子态与非线性经典场论（Gross-Pitaevskii）中孤子解之间的严格对应，理解量子涨落、退相干与经典极限之间的关系，特别是密度凹陷的展宽机制与时间演化特征。

Method: 采用同时取弱耦合极限（c→0）与大系统极限（N,L→∞）但固定平均密度n̄=N/L和标度周长Λ=L√(c n̄)的双重极限；将量子态定义为yrast单空穴态的等权重叠加；对GP周期单孤子族按正则动量进行经典系综平均；解析推导无限线极限下的初始平均密度轮廓及其渐近行为（r⁻⁵衰减）；利用Bickley-Naylor函数Ki₃展开其傅里叶变换；通过数值模拟对比量子模式方差与GP傅里叶系数二阶矩；借助yrast跃迁频率分析时间尺度分离。

Result: 1) 在标度极限下，量子归一化密度演化由正则动量加权的GP孤子系综平均精确描述；2) 解析得到无限线极限初始平均轮廓，其密度亏损满足|x|⁻⁵幂律衰减；3) 其傅里叶变换可表示为∑ₙ aₙ Ki₃(kₙx)形式；4) 量子密度涨落的长时模式方差与GP解傅里叶系数的二阶矩成正比；5) yrast跃迁频率明确区分两个动力学时标：初始退相干时间τ_dephase ∼ ℏ/(ΔE_yrast) 与涨落平均时间τ_avg ≫ τ_dephase。

Conclusion: Lieb-Liniger量子暗孤子在标度弱耦合大系统极限下，其宏观密度演化并非趋近单一GP孤子，而是正则动量空间上的GP孤子系综平均；该统计结构内在解释了凹陷展宽现象；量子涨落的统计特性由GP解的谱结构决定，揭示了可积量子系统向经典非线性场论过渡中‘量子-经典对应’的新层次——即系综平均而非单轨迹对应；yrast能谱直接编码动力学时标分离，为实验观测退相干与涨落弛豫提供理论判据。

Abstract: We study the time evolution of quantum dark-soliton states of the Lieb--Liniger model, introduced by Sato--Kanamoto--Kaminishi--Deguchi as equal-weight superpositions of one-hole yrast states, and their relation to Gross--Pitaevskii (GP) solitons. We consider a simultaneous weak-coupling and large-system limit in which the repulsive coupling constant $c$ tends to zero and the particle number $N$ and ring circumference $L$ tend to infinity, with the mean density $\bar n=N/L$ and scaled circumference $\Lambda=L\sqrt{c\bar n}$ fixed. We conjecture that, in this limit, the normalized quantum density and its evolution at fixed scaled time $\tau=c\bar n t$ are described by a canonical-momentum average of normalized periodic one-soliton GP densities, each evolving at its own velocity. We explicitly parametrize the GP family by canonical momentum and provide numerical evidence for this correspondence. The different velocities explain the spreading of the averaged density notch despite shape preservation by individual solitons. In the infinite-line limit, we derive an analytic expression for the initial averaged profile, establish an inverse-fifth-power decay of its density deficit with distance from the soliton center, and express the Fourier transform of its deviation from the background as a series of Bickley--Naylor functions $\operatorname{Ki}_3$. Following the spreading of the initial notch, we investigate temporal density fluctuations of the quantum dark solitons and propose a relation between long-time mode variances and second moments of GP Fourier coefficients, supported by numerical comparisons of individual mode variances and the total variance. Yrast transition frequencies distinguish the initial dephasing time scale from the longer scale associated with finite-time averaging of fluctuations.

</details>

*Sam Kault*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消除parafermion算符乘积展开中可能出现的更高阶奇异性项，并确认主导贡献的多项式指数满足加性结构。

Method: 采用组合数学方法，基于Faà di Bruno公式构造显式双射或计数论证，实现对卷积恒等式的严格证明。

Result: 严格证明了该卷积恒等式，确认了奇异项的精确相消机制，并确立了主导项中多项式指标的加性叠加规律。

Conclusion: 该恒等式是parafermion算符代数中OPE（算符乘积展开）收敛性与结构性的关键支撑；本组合证明不仅验证了既有结果，还拓展了其适用范围与物理诠释基础。

Abstract: We give a detailed combinatorial proof of a Faà di Bruno convolution identity used in parafermion operator-product calculations. The identity establishes the cancellation of potentially more singular terms and shows that the leading contribution combines the polynomial indices additively. The proof, originating in the author's doctoral thesis, provides a general derivation of an identity stated in earlier parafermion literature.

</details>


### [7] [Block decimation and perfect actions for $p$-adic valued path integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25770)
*Su Hu,Min-Soo Kim*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在p进数域C_p上定义的波函数的有限划分传播子，通过原子测度积分中间位置，并利用Fréchet导数描述自由作用量下的运动学减并；指出在p进框架下，实数意义上的紫外/红外尺度变换角色互换；对Z_p上解析势，加权与非加权情形下分别给出完美作用量收敛性与发散性结论；对谐振子模型，共形化（coarsening）下变分流可解析求解，得到质量重正化公式及线性化谱p^{2−b}，揭示实数与p进物理中相关/无关方向的对偶性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建适配p进数时空结构的量子力学路径积分形式，弥合理数域Q_p与实数域R之间重整化群流与尺度协变性的概念鸿沟，尤其关注经典完美作用量的p进类比及其变分减并的数学实现。

Method: 采用有限划分传播子框架，以原子测度替代Lebesgue测度进行中间点积分；将迭代积分解释为等距链上的作用量求值；运用Fréchet导数刻画自由作用量附近的运动学减并；结合Volkenborn积分、Chebyshev递推、p进正弦函数sin_p及多项式作用量空间上的线性化谱分析，系统研究 refinement（细化）与 coarsening（粗化）两种RG流向。

Result: 1) 对Z_p上幂级数势，无时间步权重时，经M次细化后作用量一致收敛于尺度协变（完美）作用量，误差为p^{-M}，对角邻域由Volkenborn积分主导；加权时无极限。2) 谐振子在固定频率下无细化极限，但在粗化下变分流闭式可解：格点角乘p、耦合满足Chebyshev递推、无焦散、重正化质量m = m_0 sin_p(θ_0)/θ_0。3) 线性化映射在多项式作用量空间可对角化，本征值为p^{2−b}，表明R与Q_p中RG相关/无关方向严格互逆。

Conclusion: p进路径积分中的尺度变换具有内禀对偶性：细化↔粗化、UV↔IR、相关↔无关方向均与实数情形互换；完美作用量的存在性高度依赖于离散测度选取（如是否含时间步权重）与势函数的p进解析性质；谐振子作为可解模型证实了p进RG流可规避实数中典型的奇异性（如caustics），并给出显式重正化公式。

Abstract: In earlier work we introduced a finite-partition propagator for $\mathbb{C}_p$-valued wave functions, in which the intermediate positions are integrated against atomic measures. The iterated integral evaluates the action on the equally spaced chain between the endpoints, so each partition level is obtained by iterating an exact, tree-level block decimation of two-point lattice actions, with no integration over fluctuations. This kinematic decimation is the Fréchet derivative, at the free actions, of the variational decimation underlying classical perfect actions. Since the time step $p^n$ is small in the real norm exactly when it is large $p$-adically, refinement and coarsening (the ultraviolet and infrared directions over $\mathbb{R}$) exchange roles, and each yields one limit. Under refinement, potentials supported on $\mathbb{Z}_p$, given there by a convergent power series, and entering the slice action without the time-step weight produce an interaction that stabilizes off the diagonal after finitely many steps and converges uniformly on $\mathbb{Z}_p^2$, with sharp error $p^{-M}$ after $M$ steps, to a scale-covariant (perfect) action, governed near the diagonal by a Volkenborn integral; with the weight no limit exists. For the harmonic oscillator no refinement limit exists at fixed frequency, but under coarsening the variational flow is solved in closed form: the lattice angle is multiplied by $p$ at each step, the coupling obeys a Chebyshev recursion, no caustic occurs, and the rescaled action converges to a free action with renormalized mass $m_0\sin_p(\theta_0)/\theta_0$. Abstract: In the pure quadratic model of the Böhmer--Jensko bulk--boundary formulation of gravity, every static metric with a regular centre has vanishing central density and pressure, so no affine equation of state admits a regular non-vacuum star. We therefore study singular cores, in scale-invariant variables in which the isotropy condition is a single autonomous relation between two functions. Closures that keep the self-similar solutions as equilibria fix the stability of two vacuum lines but leave that of the matter-bearing equilibria undetermined, except at one unphysical point, so only an equation of state yields definite dynamics. For a linear equation of state the closed system has a focus, a saddle, and a vacuum point with nonzero bulk scalar that exchanges stability with the saddle in a transcritical bifurcation. At the radiation value the system is integrable, through the Tolman integral and scale invariance, and the focus becomes a nonlinear centre surrounded by log-periodic solutions. Radiation is also exactly where the line containing the matter-bearing equilibria of this closure coincides with the line of circular null orbits. Consequently every periodic radiation core with monotonic areal radius has exactly one stable and one unstable light ring per period, at radii forming two interlaced geometric progressions towards the centre with a common ratio tending to 2.165 for small oscillations. Abstract: We develop numerical relativity evolution schemes based on Plebanski's chiral formulation of general relativity and its 3+1 form, Ashtekar's variables. Lifting this gauge fixing to its minimal nonlinear extension and performing the 3+1 decomposition yields a strongly hyperbolic modification of Ashtekar's evolution equations that remains polynomial in the fields and includes constraint damping. We also introduce a conformal (unimodular) reformulation in which the nonlinear 12-component sector evolves completely independently of the remaining fields. All three systems are tested with gauge-wave convergence tests, robust-stability tests on a noisy Minkowski background, and long-time evolutions. The unmodified Ashtekar system crashes at T ~ 325, whereas the gauge-fixed hyperbolic and conformal systems remain stable up to T = 2000, with constraint violations and the imaginary part of the metric remaining at the 10^{-10} level.

Conclusion: TRUFFLE是首个满足LISA MBHB多阶段电磁协同观测全部核心要求（实时性、校准性、鲁棒性）的端到端仿真推断框架，为未来多信使天文学提供关键算法支撑。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) will observe massive black hole binaries (MBHBs) for days to months before merger, offering opportunities for advance electromagnetic follow-up. To enable such observations, inference methods must provide rapid, continuously updated, and statistically calibrated posterior estimates, while remaining robust to overlapping sources. We present TRUFFLE (Time-evolving Rapid Updates For Follow-up of LISA Events), a simulation-based inference method for MBHBs in LISA data designed to meet these requirements. Our approach uses two networks, selected according to the time to coalescence relative to the end of the analysis window, $\Delta t_c$. An early-warning network, operating at $\Delta t_c \in [4~\mathrm{h}, 2~\mathrm{d}]$, estimates the chirp mass, mass ratio, and coalescence time to support electromagnetic follow-up scheduling. A near-merger network, operating at $\Delta t_c \in [-1~\mathrm{h}, +4~\mathrm{h}]$, infers nine parameters, including the sky position needed for telescope pointing. Both networks process the most recent six days of data using multiband time- and frequency-domain representations, which are summarized by a transformer to condition a normalizing flow. The networks are trained on the full orbit-driven LISA response and amortized over a 30-day range of observation epochs. The near-merger posterior can be corrected through likelihood-based importance sampling, with neural inference taking seconds and likelihood reweighting approximately five minutes on a single GPU. We obtain unbiased posteriors in the presence of overlapping sources, including plunging extreme-mass-ratio inspirals, resolvable Galactic binaries, and additional MBHBs merging up to two hours before the primary merger.

Abstract: We reconstruct the magnetic nonlinear electrodynamics (NED) source of the Dymnikova geometry on the branch \(\Iinv>0\) and analyze its electromagnetic perturbations on the fixed background. The reconstruction does not admit a Maxwell limit. The extraordinary characteristic cone gives a transverse speed \(v_\perp^2=(3u-5)/2\), with \(u=r^3/P^3\), which becomes superluminal for \(r>r_{\rm lum}=(7/3)^{1/3}P\). The principal system is pointwise hyperbolic in the finite exterior \(r\ge r_e\), with positive local quadratic energy, but not uniformly hyperbolic up to spatial infinity. The radial effective potential admits an exact reduction, \(V_{\rm em}=f\ell(\ell+1)\mathcal{A}/r^2+ff_{,r}q +f^2(q_{,r}+\frac34q^2)\), behaves as \(V_0\propto\epsilon^2\) near the horizon, and grows as \(27r^4/(4P^6)\) at infinity. It is numerically positive on a fine grid up to \(r=50P\). The Dymnikova reconstruction does not satisfy the hypotheses of the Tomizawa--Suzuki theorem, since it lacks a Maxwell limit, yet it still produces superluminal extraordinary photons. The reconstructed NED branch is therefore mathematically consistent as a source of the Dymnikova background, but not causally acceptable with respect to the physical metric in the asymptotic region. The global positivity of the effective potential and the quasinormal spectrum are left as open problems.

Abstract: This work presents measurements of the total and single-differential electron neutrino charged-current cross sections on argon using the Short-Baseline Near Detector (SBND) at Fermilab. We present the highest statistics inclusive $\nu_e + \bar \nu_e$ (95.2% $\nu_e$ and 4.8% $\bar\nu_e$) charged-current cross section measurement on argon to date and the first inclusive $\nu_e + \bar \nu_e$ cross section measurement using the Booster Neutrino Beam. Direct measurements of this channel are crucial for future studies of both long- and short-baseline neutrino oscillations through electron neutrino appearance, which in turn are vital for answering open questions in neutrino phenomenology and searching for beyond the Standard Model physics at the intensity frontier. The data used for this work were collected with an exposure of $8.81 \times10^{19}$ protons on target, and 729 candidate events are selected with a minimum electron energy of 500 MeV. The single-differential cross sections are extracted in electron energy and angle with respect to the beam axis. The flux-averaged total cross section is measured to be $\sigma = [3.90 \pm 0.18\,\text{(stat)} \pm 0.41\,\text{(syst)}] \times 10^{-39}$ cm$^2$/nucleon for a predicted average neutrino energy of $\langle E_\nu \rangle=1.8$ GeV for selected events. The total and differential cross sections are compared to predictions from common neutrino event generators. The data show a preference for generators that predict a lower overall cross section.

Abstract: Measurements of charm and beauty production in proton--proton (pp) collisions provide crucial tests of perturbative quantum chromodynamics (pQCD) and unique access to heavy-quark hadronisation. In this contribution, a selection of recent ALICE results, with emphasis on LHC Run 3 measurements, is presented. The transverse-momentum-differential production cross sections of prompt D mesons at $\sqrt{s}=5.36$ and 13.6 TeV and of fully reconstructed B mesons at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$ TeV are discussed together with comparisons to theoretical calculations. The rapidity dependence of charm and beauty production and the $\rm D^{*+}/D^0$ and $\rm \Lambda_c^+/D^0$ yield ratios provide complementary constraints on heavy-quark production and hadronisation. Measurements of the fractions of ${\rm J}/\psi$ and $\psi({\rm 2S})$ originating from beauty-hadron decays, and of the inclusive $\psi({\rm 2S})$-to-${\rm J}/\psi$ production ratio, further constrain the interplay between perturbative heavy-quark production and non-perturbative bound-state formation.

The ratio of this branching fraction to that of the corresponding radiative decay $J/\psi \to \gamma\eta$ is determined to be $\mathcal{B}(J/\psi \to \mu^+\mu^-\eta)/\mathcal{B}(J/\psi \to \gamma\eta) = (2.80 \pm 0.18) \times 10^{-3}$. Combining this result with the previous BESIII measurement of the $J/\psi \to e^+e^-\eta$ branching fraction yields $\mathcal{B}(J/\psi \to \mu^+\mu^-\eta)/\mathcal{B}(J/\psi \to e^+e^-\eta) = 0.219 \pm 0.020$. These results are consistent with the theoretical predictions based on the vector meson dominance model.

Abstract: The growing availability of open neutrino data promises broader scrutiny of interaction models, but a critical barrier remains: mapping theoretical predictions into reconstructed observables, which previously required computationally intensive methods inaccessible outside the original experimental collaborations. In this work, we present a probabilistic surrogate of the MINERvA detector response, trained on an open simulation dataset to map generator-level final-state particles and interaction geometry to reconstructed observables, without relying on generator-specific interaction labels. We further demonstrate generalization beyond the limitations of conventional phase-space-dependent reweighting by withholding two-particle-two-hole interactions entirely from training and showing that the surrogate reproduces their reconstructed response within the estimated uncertainties. In an end-to-end application to MINERvA charged-current inclusive muon-neutrino data, we reproduce selection efficiencies and purities and forward-fold predictions from GENIE, NuWro, and GiBUU for direct comparison with measurements. By moving theoretical predictions into reconstructed space rather than unfolding the data, this work establishes a reusable interface between neutrino interaction models and experimental observations, expanding the scope of physics accessible through open datasets.

Abstract: The phenomenon of confinement is arguably the key feature of gauge theories regularly studied in high-energy and condensed matter physics. This causes hadrons to exist as stable particles in quantum chromodynamics (QCD) as bound states of quarks (and gluons), and strings in pure gauge theories. The promise of realizing such strings in quantum simulator setups have fueled extensive studies of a plethora of simpler lattice gauge theories (LGTs). Quantum link models (QLMs) generalize Wilsonian LGTs and yield richer scenarios of confinement. For example, the string joining two static charges in the spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ $U(1)$ QLM fractionalize, giving rise to confining phases that break the point group and internal symmetries spontaneously. It was further shown in PRL 130, 071901 (2023) that adding annealed $Q = \pm 2$ charges to this model qualitatively changes the confinement-deconfinement transition. In this paper, we examine the finite temperature phase transition both in the presence and the absence of annealed charges through detailed extraction of critical temperature, $T_c$ , and exponents. The (effective) string tension $\sigma$ at zero and finite- temperatures are compared between the theory with and without annealed charges. The quantity $T_c/\sqrt{\sigma}$ for the QLM with annealed charges is similar to the usual $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge theory, while the theory without the charges provides a new universal value.

Abstract: Gluons play an essential role in the strong interaction that binds quarks into hadrons, as described by quantum chromodynamics (QCD). Understanding how the fundamental dynamics of QCD give rise to hadron structure remains one of the outstanding challenges in nuclear and particle physics. Parton distribution functions (PDFs) provide a successful framework for describing high-energy scattering processes involving hadrons. However, our knowledge of gluon PDFs remains comparatively limited because of the experimental challenges associated with directly constraining the gluon structure of hadrons. In recent years, a major breakthrough has enabled the calculation of the Bjorken-$x$ dependence of PDFs directly from lattice QCD. Among the available approaches, the large-momentum effective theory (LaMET) and pseudo-PDF frameworks have emerged as the most widely used methods and have produced the majority of lattice determinations of parton distributions. These developments have sparked rapid progress in first-principles studies of the $x$-dependent structure of hadrons, including gluon PDFs of the nucleon and pseudoscalar mesons. In this review, we summarize recent advances in lattice QCD calculations of gluon PDFs {using the LaMET and pseudo-PDF methods}, discuss the current status and impacts of these developments, and highlight the remaining challenges and future opportunities.

The target dependence is encoded in a finite-momentum electromagnetic transition current and, for charge-dominated response, in a qubit transition form factor. We find that the resulting enhancement substantially improves the projected sensitivity at the lowest masses. Owing to the exceptionally low transition threshold of superconducting qubits, this approach opens sensitivity to dark matter in the eV-to-keV mass range, which remains largely unexplored by existing direct-detection experiments. We further show that $N$ independently operated qubits give the expected rate proportional to $N$, while suitably prepared Dicke states can in principle achieve a collective enhancement approaching $N^2$ when spatial coherence is maintained across the array. These results establish superconducting qubits as quantum targets for direct particle scattering and provide a framework for optimizing low-threshold dark-matter searches.

Abstract: Popular frameworks that explain the baryon asymmetry of the Universe (BAU) are electroweak baryogenesis, requiring a first-order phase transition, and Majorana leptogenesis, which relies on lepton number violation in the neutrino sector. Dirac leptogenesis offers an alternative mechanism that avoids both requirements, instead allowing the BAU to follow from previously generated chiral asymmetries that remain out of equilibrium in the early Universe. Conventional realizations of this scenario typically invoke ultra-light Dirac neutrinos. In this work, we generalize the mechanism to include Standard Model charged leptons by assuming that their mass-generating interactions have a UV origin and arise from higher-dimensional operators. For concreteness, the analysis is performed within a Froggatt-Nielsen setup, which allows us to naturally address fermionic mass hierarchies, too, now linked to the BAU, and we show that the proposed mechanism is viable by supercooling the flavon phase transition. The framework predicts a flavon with a (1.7-50) GeV mass, bounded by the non-observation of lepton flavor violation and the requirement for the Universe to not reheat above sphaleron temperatures, which could be indirectly probed by future lepton flavor violation experiments.

Abstract: We present the complete next-to-next-to-leading-order (NNLO) QCD corrections to radiative and inclusive hadronic $\Upsilon(1S)$ decays in the color-singlet framework at leading order in the heavy-quark velocity expansion. Abstract: We for the first time investigate the photon-mediated endothermic axino-bino scattering process to explain the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) high-energy recoil event. We calculate the signals from non-relativistic halo and boosted dark matter (DM), including natural xenon isotopes, detector efficiency, and energy resolution. For a fixed Standard Halo Model (SHM) distribution, a simplified three-bin Poisson analysis yields a best-fit benchmark with mass $m\simeq506\,\mathrm{GeV}$ and mass splitting $\delta\simeq326\,\mathrm{keV}$ at the adopted upper coupling bound $g_\gamma=10^{-3}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$. It predicts approximately one accepted event, with $70\%$ in the middle bin. A boosted benchmark with $m\simeq82.87\,\mathrm{GeV}$ and $\delta\simeq0.626\,\mathrm{MeV}$ provides a comparable fit. Abstract: We present updated recommendations of the LHC Higgs Working Group (LHCHWG) for the inclusive production cross sections of a Standard Model Higgs boson in proton--proton collisions at centre-of-mass energies between $7$ and $14$ TeV, including the Run-3 energy of $13.6$ TeV, and for Higgs-boson masses between $120$ and $130$ GeV. All the major production modes are covered. The central values remain consistent with those of Yellow Report 4 (YR4) [arXiv:1610.07922] within the uncertainties quoted there, while the theoretical uncertainties are reduced for most production modes. The improvements rest on specific calculations rather than on a uniform advance: the complete N$^3$LO result in the heavy-top limit together with exact NNLO top-quark mass effects for gluon fusion; the N$^3$LO structure-function calculation supplemented by non-factorisable corrections for vector-boson fusion; the completion of the NLO QCD corrections to the loop-induced $gg\to ZH$ channel for $VH$; NNLO QCD matched to NNLL soft-gluon resummation and combined with NLO electroweak corrections for $t\bar{t}H$, and updated predictions for $tH$; and the matched four- and five-flavour-scheme prediction, with its explicit resummation-scale uncertainty, for $b\bar{b}H$. Since no PDF4LHC recommendation exists beyond NNLO, the mismatch between an N$^3$LO matrix element and NNLO distributions has to be carried as an uncertainty, estimated process by process: it is well determined for vector-boson fusion, whereas for gluon fusion it is doubled relative to YR4. PDFs are now the leading source of uncertainty for the most precisely predicted modes, while residual perturbative uncertainties dominate for the remainder. These results supersede the inclusive cross sections of YR4 and the ad interim recommendations of Ref. [arXiv:2402.09955], and form part of Report 5 of the LHCHWG.

Abstract: Methods used in mechanistic interpretability have revealed human-aligned concepts inside language and image models. In this paper, we search for physics concepts inside a neural a generative collider physics model trained on real LHC data (ShellFlow). We discover features inside ShellFlow that represent resonances, such as the $Z$, $J/\psi$, and $\Upsilon$. The network was never told which muons come from a resonance, it was only trained to generate the kinematics of the final-state particles. We use these features to alter aspects of the $Z$ boson: we change its mass between $\sim80$ and $105$ GeV, and removing such a feature from the neural network produces a broader $Z$ peak. In both cases the rest of the invariant mass spectrum remains mostly unchanged. The feature was built from the gradients of the neural network, and its selectivity was built using pattern recognition methods. A generative particle physics model trained on unlabeled data can be modified internally to change a physical quantity.

Abstract: Reliable predictions for the electroweak phase transition depend critically on a consistent resummation of thermal fluctuations. However, existing prescriptions often entail sizable uncertainties, motivating the development of a robust and systematically controlled resummation scheme. In this work, we develop an improved partial dressing (IPD) scheme that resolves intrinsic inconsistencies in the original partial dressing prescription, including the overlapping momentum problem and the associated divergences in the gap equations. By consistently incorporating momentum-dependent self-energies and reorganizing the daisy resummation, the IPD scheme provides a more systematic framework for thermal resummation. We perform a comprehensive comparison of 5 different resummation prescriptions in the well-motivated inert doublet model and demonstrate that the choice of resummation scheme can substantially affect the predicted phase transition parameters and the resulting gravitational wave spectra. Notably, the IPD prescription yields a reliable potential and comparatively robust phenomenological predictions, highlighting the importance of a self-consistent treatment of thermal corrections. Our results establish the theoretical consistency of IPD and demonstrate that controlling resummation uncertainties is essential for reliable predictions of cosmological phase transitions and their gravitational wave signatures.

</details>

Method: 基于修改Zbb̄耦合所诱导的有效接触相互作用（contact operators），分析其对pp→Zh和pp→Zhb末态的干涉效应；结合ATLAS对pp→Zh截面的精确测量，进行统计显著性检验。

Abstract: Constraining the $Z$-boson couplings of the $b$ quark, or even ruling out the possibility of an opposite-sign coupling, is considered very challenging at the LHC. We also argue that $pp\to Zhb$, which receives a contribution from the same contact term, would lead to even better sensitivity. Furthermore, we show that with future data this indirect method can provide the strongest constraints on $Zb\bar{b}$ couplings, and can even probe the anomaly in these couplings left over from the LEP era.

Abstract: Superradiant clouds of ultralight scalars around black holes are often modeled as free fields, whose levels grow independently and decay slowly through gravitational-wave emission. Abstract: Observed neutrino oscillations can be reproduced by a refractive neutrino mass-squared arising from interactions with ultralight scalar dark matter (ULDM). The wave behaviour of the scalar field gives rise to a fluctuating interference pattern, inducing spatial and temporal variations in the refractive mass that can distort neutrino oscillation probabilities. We simulate the interference patterns of the scalar field in the presence of self-interactions. Using results of these simulations, the oscillation probabilities for JUNO and T2K experiments are computed. We find that the refractive mass scenario remains viable for the complex scalar field without self-coupling for $m_\phi \lesssim 10^{-16}~\mathrm{eV}$. For $m_\phi\gtrsim10^{-16}~\mathrm{eV}$, repulsive self-interactions narrow the density distribution, reducing the deviation of the survival probability at the first minimum to $\Delta P \approx 0.07$ for an appropriate self-interaction coupling. In contrast, for a real scalar field, rapid oscillations with the Compton frequency distort the probabilities for scalar masses $ 10^{-22}~\mathrm{eV} \lesssim m_\phi \lesssim 10^{-10} ~\mathrm{eV}$, and self-interactions do not remove this distortion. This distortion becomes negligible only for $m_\phi\gtrsim10^{-9}~\mathrm{eV}$. We explore additional effects which could modify the interference pattern and relax the constraints on the required values of $m_\phi$. These include scenarios with multiple scalar fields (the axiverse), the gravitational effects of the Sun and Earth on the granule structure, and interactions with the relic neutrino background.

Abstract: Determining the absolute neutrino mass scale remains a compelling challenge in particle physics. Establishing precise mathematical connections among the neutrino masses could significantly facilitate this task and shed additional light on the underlying mass-generation mechanism. We highlight the possible existence of a simple algebraic relation between the mass-squared differences, $$\frac{\sqrt{\Delta m^2_{31}}+\sqrt{\Delta m^2_{21}}}{\sqrt{\Delta m^2_{31}}-\sqrt{\Delta m^2_{21}}}=\sqrt{2},$$ which agrees remarkably well with the best-fit values of recent global analyses of neutrino oscillation data. We then investigate the implications of adopting this relation and how it decreases the number of independent parameters in the neutrino sector. We demonstrate that there are alternative representations well known in quark and charged-lepton physics. Furthermore, we place a tight constraint on the solar neutrino mass splitting, $\Delta m^2_{21}=(7.445 \pm 0.062) \times 10^{-5} \text{ eV}^2$, and also obtain $\Delta m^2_{31}=(2.548 \pm 0.041) \times 10^{-3} \text{ eV}^2$. In particular, the Jiangmen Underground Neutrino Observatory (JUNO) will measure these parameters independently of each other at the sub-percent level, thereby providing a stringent and direct test of our results.

Abstract: M-theory local models may exhibit torsional cycles and other non-trivial topology which interpolate between quantum field theory sectors localized at singularities and the asymptotic regions of the local model. We show that such structures give rise, in standard decoupling limits, to additional topological sectors which can couple non-trivially to the localized QFT. From a symmetry topological field theory (SymTFT) perspective, the physical boundary conditions associated to such local models are modified. *Shiroman Prakash,Shubham Kumar Sinha*

TL;DR: 本文在d=4−ε维度下，对一般可重整标量-费米子理论的任意不动点，计算了应力张量和守恒流两点函数系数CT与CJ至ε³阶；通过N=1超对称性解决d≠3时奇数Yukawa顶点费米子圈的维数正规化歧义；并利用Padé逼近估算石墨烯多种量子临界点及拓扑绝缘体表面超对称相变的普适电导率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算临界理论中CT和CJ对于检验AdS/CFT对应、理解量子临界现象及普适输运性质至关重要；而现有ε展开在含费米子理论中于ε³阶面临维数正规化歧义，尤其在无四维母理论的三维强耦合体系中亟需自洽处理。

Method: 采用最小减除方案（MS）下的ε展开，结合费曼图计算至三环（ε³）；对含奇数Yukawa顶点的费米子圈，借助N=1超对称性约束其d维延拓，通过四环超流/费米型味流两点函数确定正规化方案；辅以Padé-Borel逼近提升收敛性。

Result: 获得CT、CJ至ε³阶普适表达式；给出手性Ising/XY/Heisenberg模型及三维超对称不动点的具体数值；解析确定超对称表面相变电导率σ/σfree=9/10×CT/CT,free≈0.654；石墨烯CDW、Kekulé、Néel转变的普适电导率分别约为0.91、0.79–0.83、0.64–0.74。

Conclusion: ε³阶计算揭示了费米子自由度对中心荷和规范流强度的非平凡修正；超对称性为解决维数正规化歧义提供了物理基准；所得电导率值显著修正了先前估计，为石墨烯与拓扑物态的量子临界输运提供了高精度理论预言。

Abstract: We compute $C_T$ and $C_J$, the coefficients of the stress-tensor and conserved-current two-point functions, to order $\epsilon^3$ at any fixed point of the general renormalizable theory of scalars and fermions in $d=4-\epsilon$. We give explicit results for the chiral Ising, XY and Heisenberg models and for fixed points with emergent supersymmetry in $d=3$. In theories without a four-dimensional counterpart, fermion loops with an odd number of Yukawa vertices contribute at order $\epsilon^3$ through traces whose continuation away from $d=3$ is ambiguous; we fix it with $\mathcal N=1$ supersymmetry, by computing the two-point functions of the supercurrent and of the fermionic partner of a flavor current to four loops. In $d=3$, Padé approximants closely reproduce the exact $C_T$ of the $\mathcal N=2$ super-Ising model. We estimate the universal conductivity at the charge-density-wave, Kekulé and Néel transitions of graphene to be $\sigma/\sigma_{\rm free}\approx0.91$, $0.79$--$0.83$ and $0.64$--$0.74$, and show that the exact conductivity at the supersymmetric transition on the surface of a topological insulator is $\sigma/\sigma_{\rm free}=\frac{9}{10}\,C_T/C_{T,\rm free}\approx0.654$, $10\%$ below the value previously reported.

</details>


### [95] [Nonzero cubic Love numbers of Schwarzschild black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25059)
*Bruno Bucciotti*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了Schwarzschild黑洞在广义相对论中三阶非轴对称八极潮汐响应，通过匹配世界线有效场论（EFT），发现一个既不消失也不跑动的静态Love数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解广义相对论中黑洞非线性潮汐响应，特别是高阶静态Love数是否普遍消失或具有新动力学行为。

Method: 求解三阶非轴对称八极静态潮汐扰动；利用Parra-Martinez与Podo所揭示的静态广义相对论对称性；采用维数正规化将结果匹配至世界线EFT。

Result: 发现一个非零、非跑动（即尺度无关）的三阶静态Love数，突破了线性与二次阶Love数为零的传统认知。

Conclusion: Schwarzschild黑洞在三次非线性阶存在非平凡静态潮汐响应，表明静态Love数在高阶可非零且无重整化群跑动，暗示静态黑洞存在可观测的非线性形变记忆。

Abstract: Static Love numbers of Schwarzschild black holes vanish at linear and quadratic order in General Relativity, while generic nonlinear tides remain less understood. We solve a specific non-axisymmetric octupolar tide at cubic order and, building on the symmetry of static General Relativity exploited by Parra-Martinez and Podo, match the result to a worldline effective field theory (EFT) using dimensional regularization. Surprisingly, we find a Love number that neither vanishes nor runs.

</details>


### [96] [Soft Factorisation of WQFT and the Spinning 4PM Magnusian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25090)
*Riccardo Gonzo,Gustav Mogull,Benjamin Sauer*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Worldline Quantum Field Theory (WQFT)框架下，利用Faddeev-Kulish类红外有限N-矩阵方法，系统导出了软发散结构（Coulomb与tail型），确认其源于Wilson线关联子；并基于Magnus级数与Murua系数，在4PM阶精确计算了含自旋修正的红外发散/红外有限Magnusian N，复现了动量冲量、自旋踢及散射波形的红外行为，并通过∂N/∂L给出至3PM的散射角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决广义相对论后闵可夫斯基（PM）散射中软引力辐射导致的红外发散问题，建立红外安全的散射算符描述，特别是对含自旋双体系统的高阶量子/经典对应。

Method: 采用Worldline QFT形式化，结合Faddeev-Kulish型红外重定义（构造红外有限N̂ = −i log Ŝ），将软发散归结为世界线上的Wilson线关联；运用Magnus展开及其Murua系数系统组织高阶PM贡献，并引入Feynman i0⁺规制以提取红外有限部分。

Result: 1) 明确识别并分类了4PM阶Coulomb（质量-质量）与tail（质量-无质量）型软极点；2) 验证了Δpᵢ^μ与ΔSᵢ^μ等含时可观测量的红外相消；3) 复现了一圈散射波形的红外发散；4) 给出含线性自旋修正的4PM Magnusian N，其虚部含log(ωIR)软发散，实部经i0⁺规制后编码保守动量冲量与自旋踢；5) 对共线自旋情形，−∂N/∂L在3PM内精确给出散射角。

Conclusion: WQFT + Magnus-N框架为处理含自旋双体引力散射中的红外结构提供了统一、可计算且红外可控的语言；4PM结果证实软因子化预言，并表明红外有限Magnusian可作为经典动力学（尤其散射角与守恒量）的紧凑生成泛函。

Abstract: We derive the soft divergences of $\hat{N}=-i\log\hat{S}$ in the Worldline Quantum Field Theory (WQFT) formalism, focusing on poles due to single massive-massive (Coulomb) and massive-massless (tail) graviton exchanges. Our derivation is inspired by the Faddeev-Kulish procedure, switching the interaction picture to one with an infrared-finite $\hat{N}$-matrix. Soft divergences emerging from the worldline are correlators of Wilson lines. As confirmation of this structure, we demonstrate explicitly how divergences of inclusive observables including the momentum impulse $\Delta p_i^\mu$ and spin kick $\Delta S_i^\mu$ cancel, and we reproduce the infrared divergence of the one-loop scattering waveform. Using the Magnus series representation of $\hat{N}$ and Murua coefficients, we then calculate the Magnusian $N=\langle0|\hat{N}|0\rangle$ at fourth post-Minkowskian (4PM) order, including linear-in-spin corrections. For aligned spins, $-\partial N/\partial L$ reproduces the full scattering angle through 3PM order; at 4PM order, $N$ develops a soft divergence as predicted by our soft factorisation. Using the Feynman $i0^+$ prescription, we also derive an infrared-finite 4PM Magnusian which compactly encodes existing results for the conservative momentum impulse and spin kick via nested iterations of Poisson brackets.

</details>


### [97] [Calabi-Yau linear models with abelian bundles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25101)
*Yann Proto*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一类(0,2)规范线性σ模型，其大半径相实现带有线丛的Calabi-Yau σ模型；研究了具有Landau-Ginzburg orbifold相的一族模型，该相被描述为(2,2) Gepner模型与自由左移费米子的非对称orbifold；揭示了扭曲扇区中自由费米子的涌现及左移电流代数的显著增强；GLSM将Calabi-Yau几何与这些有理点联系起来，体积模在增强规范对称性下带电并冻结于弦尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展Gepner构造至更广的(0,2)超共形场论，并理解Calabi-Yau几何与Landau-Ginzburg orbifold相之间的量子几何对应关系。

Method: (0,2)规范线性σ模型构造、非对称orbifold技术、扭曲扇区谱分析、左移N=(0,2)超对称流代数提升机制研究。

Result: 发现扭曲扇区中涌现自由左移费米子；左移电流代数发生实质性增强；体积模在增强规范对称性下带电并冻结于弦尺度；建立了Calabi-Yau sigma模型与特定(0,2) LG orbifold有理点之间的精确对应。

Conclusion: 该工作为(0,2)镜像对称与量子几何相变提供了新范例，表明规范对称性增强可导致模空间紧致化，且非对称orbifold是构造稳定(0,2)真空的重要工具。

Abstract: We introduce a construction of (0,2) gauged linear sigma models whose large radius phases realize Calabi-Yau sigma models with line bundles. We study a family of such models admitting Landau-Ginzburg orbifold phases, described as asymmetric orbifolds of (2,2) Gepner models and free left-moving fermions. These orbifolds extend the Gepner construction to a broader class of (0,2) SCFTs, and reveal unexpected features, including the emergence of free fermions in twisted sectors and substantial enhancements of the left-moving current algebra. The gauged linear sigma model connects the Calabi-Yau geometry to these rational points, where the volume modulus becomes charged under the enhanced gauge symmetry and freezes at the string scale.

</details>


### [98] [From Instantons to AdS Boundaries: Tracing the Geometric Path from Quantum Anomalies to Massive Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25103)
*Leonardo Sanhueza*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究边界数据如何影响两类引力可观测量：引力瞬子上的Dirac指标和三维New Massive Gravity中的守恒荷。在Einstein-ModMax引力中，利用Atiyah-Patodi-Singer定理计算了Taub-NUT与Eguchi-Hanson瞬子上带电Dirac费米子的指标；在New Massive Gravity中，通过引入外曲率项重构全息应力张量，并在退化点分析松弛边界条件下的Fefferman-Graham展开与抵消残余发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解引力瞬子上量子场论反常与全息守恒荷对边界数据的依赖性，尤其在非线性电磁理论（ModMax）与高阶引力理论（NMG）中边界自由度的系统刻画。

Method: 应用Atiyah-Patodi-Singer指标定理计算带边界的Dirac算子指标；采用Brown-Henneaux边界条件与Fefferman-Graham展开分析AdS₃中NMG的渐近结构；结合Noether-Wald泛函与全息重整化推导守恒荷与Ward恒等式。

Result: 1) ModMax修正不改变Taub-NUT指标，但Eguchi-Hanson指标中的轴向反常显含ModMax参数；2) NMG中线性外曲率项复现辅助场形式的全息应力张量；3) 退化点存在受约束的额外FG系数，二次外曲率抵消项消除剩余幂律发散；4) 全息荷与Noether-Wald荷一致。

Conclusion: 边界数据通过谱不对称性（APS定理）与渐近几何（FG展开）深刻影响引力可观测量；ModMax参数与NMG质量参数分别调控反常结构与边界自由度计数，验证了广义引力框架下全息守恒律的普适性。

Abstract: This thesis studies how boundary data enter two gravitational observables: the Dirac index on gravitational instantons and conserved charges in three-dimensional New Massive Gravity. In Einstein gravity coupled to ModMax electrodynamics, we compute the index of charged Dirac fermions on Taub-NUT and Eguchi-Hanson instantons using the Atiyah-Patodi-Singer theorem, which includes the spectral asymmetry of the boundary Dirac operator. The Taub-NUT index agrees with the Maxwell result. For Eguchi-Hanson, the gauge-field contribution to the axial anomaly depends explicitly on the ModMax parameter when expressed in terms of the solution parameters. For asymptotically anti-de Sitter solutions of New Massive Gravity with Brown-Henneaux boundary conditions, a term linear in the extrinsic curvature reproduces the holographic stress tensor of the auxiliary-field formulation. At the degenerate point, relaxed boundary conditions admit an additional constrained Fefferman-Graham coefficient. A quadratic extrinsic counterterm cancels the residual power divergence. We derive the boundary Ward identity and show that the holographic and Noether-Wald charges agree for the solutions studied.

</details>


### [99] [Holographic Duals of $SL^{(q)}(2, \mathbb{R})$ Deformed CFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25240)
*Somnath Porey,Baishali Roy,Rahul Roy,Bobby Ezhuthachan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了由sl^(q)(2,R)-变形哈密顿量驱动的2D CFT的全息体对偶，构建了对应于加热相、非加热相及相边界三类动力学的Bañados几何，并从因果视界与测地线结构解释了纠缠熵的线性增长及OTOC的指数增长条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非平衡量子多体系统中热化与信息传播的全息刻画，特别是sl^(q)(2,R)形变下Floquet驱动CFT的体对偶几何结构及其信息动力学表征。

Method: 在Hamiltonian-adapted参考系中构造sl^(q)(2,R)-变形Floquet有效哈密顿量；求解对应Bañados型AdS₃度规；分析固定点结构、因果视界演化与测地线穿透行为；结合全局AdS与商空间（quotient）描述，对比解释纠缠熵与OTOC的动力学。

Result: 1) 加热相中两相邻不动点间体区域存在时依赖因果视界，且晚时渐近于静态AdS-Rindler几何；非加热相无视界；2) 纠缠熵线性增长可由全局AdS中穿越视界的测地线段或商空间中 boosted conical defect 诱导的有效shockwave解释；3) q=1时，从体计算精确复现CFT OTOC，并证明其指数增长仅当算符位于两个不动点同侧时发生。

Conclusion: sl^(q)(2,R)形变Floquet CFT的全息对偶由具有特征不动点结构的Bañados几何精确刻画，其相结构直接决定体因果结构与信息传播性质，为共形动力学与量子混沌的几何化提供了严格范例。

Abstract: We study the holographic bulk dual of 2D CFTs evolved by $sl^{(q)}(2,R)$-deformed Hamiltonians. Working in the Hamiltonian-adapted frame, in which the $sl^{(q)}(2,R)$-deformed effective Floquet Hamiltonian generates time translations, we construct the dual Bañados geometries for the three distinct classes of dynamics: the heating phase, the non-heating phase and the phase boundary. Specifically, we show that in the heating phase, the bulk patch dual to the region between two neighbouring fixed points has a time-dependent causal horizon and asymptotes to a static AdS Rindler geometry at late times, while the non-heating phase has no horizon. We discuss how the late-time behaviour of the entanglement entropy, particularly its linear growth in the heating phase, can be understood from the bulk in two different ways: in global AdS, from the portion of the geodesic behind the horizons, and in a quotient description, from an effective shockwave produced by a boosted conical defect. Finally, for ($q=1$), we complete the holographic analysis of Ref. [1] by reproducing the exact CFT expression of the OTOC from the bulk and explaining why it exhibits an exponential growth {\it only} when the operators lie on the side of the two fixed points.

</details>


### [100] [Upper Bounds on the Radion Mass in a Warped Extra Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25473)
*Leandro Da Rold,Eugenio Megias,Mariano Quiros,Lindber Salas*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究五维弯曲时空模型中轻子态径向模（radion）与引力子模（graviton）质量比 $R_{11}=m_{r_1}/m_{h_1}$ 的绝对上限，发现该比值与红外奇异性处的里奇标量发散行为密切相关；对幂律型和指数型体势分别给出严格约束，尤其证明在指数型情形下 $R_{11}<1$，且第二径向模比 $R_{21} \lesssim 1.374$，结果满足幺正性求和规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定 warped 五维模型中 radion 与 graviton 轻子态质量比的普适上界，以检验模型的自洽性、幺正性及与实验可观测性的兼容性，特别是针对红外奇点附近允许的非正则（singular）背景几何。

Method: 基于五维爱因斯坦-标量耦合系统，分析具有幂律 $\sim \bar\phi^{2n}$ 和指数 $\sim e^{2\nu\bar\phi}$ 体势的稳定化背景解；通过解析求解径向和引力子 Kaluza-Klein 谱，结合 Ricci 标量在 IR 奇点处的渐近行为，导出质量比的紧致上界；利用幺正性求和规则验证边界构型的理论一致性。

Result: 对指数型势，严格证明 $R_{11} < 1$，极限 $\nu \to 1^+$ 时趋近该界；幂律型势中 $R_{11}^{\rm max}$ 随 $n$ 增大而增大，并在 $n\to\infty$ 时收敛至同一界；对第二径向模，得绝对上限 $R_{21} \lesssim 1.374$，于 $\nu=2$ 且 IR 剪切趋近奇点时取等；完整 radion KK 塔满足幺正性求和规则。

Conclusion: 径向-引力子质量比存在由时空几何奇异性决定的绝对上界，指数型势给出最紧致约束 $R_{11}<1$，表明轻 radion 不可能重于轻 graviton；该结果为 Randall-Sundrum 类模型的紫外完备性与 LHC 等实验探测窗口提供关键理论基准。

Abstract: We study absolute upper bounds on the mass ratio $R_{11}=m_{r_1}/m_{h_1}$ between the radion ($r_1$) and graviton ($h_1$) lightest modes in five dimensional warped models. We consider warped models with an admissible singular behavior in the infrared: i.e.~models with power-like ($\sim \bar\phi^{2n}$) and with exponential ($\sim e^{2\nu\bar\phi}$) bulk potentials, where $\bar\phi$ is the stabilizing bulk field. We find a correlation between $R_{11}$ and the singular behavior of the Ricci scalar, which allows to establish an absolute upper bound on the former. For the power-like models, the maximum value of $R_{11}$ increases with $n$. In the limit $n\to\infty$, the power-like model reproduces the exponential model and reaches the same upper bound as the latter. For the exponential model, we derive analytically the bound $R_{11}<1$, with the largest value approached for the case $\nu\to 1^+$ (where $\nu=1$ corresponds to the linear dilaton model) and consistent with unitarity sum rules. For exponential models we also obtain the absolute upper bound on the second radion mode ratio $R_{21}=m_{r_2}/m_{h_1}\lesssim 1.374$. This value is reached for $\nu=2$ when the IR brane approaches the singularity. For this configuration, we verify that the scalar contribution satisfies the unitarity sum rule when the complete radion Kaluza-Klein tower is included.

</details>


### [101] [Pairwise fermionic mode correlations in electric and magnetic backgrounds: Landau-level structure and Schwinger production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25507)
*Sahil Chetan Patnekar,Sristi Sanjivanee Goswami,Sanjay Pant,Shagun Kaushal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在平行电场和磁场中费米子Schwinger对产生过程中的互信息与对数负性，通过自旋依赖的Bogoliubov变换构建in-vacuum态，分析Landau模式下的粒子-反粒子关联，并推广至Sauter型电场情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强场量子电动力学中真空纠缠结构随外场（特别是电-磁复合场）的演化规律，尤其是Landau能级结构对关联度量的影响。

Method: 采用自旋依赖的Bogoliubov变换构造in-vacuum；显式区分同一物理Landau能级内不同自旋分量的模式；对六种 bipartition 进行互信息与对数负性计算；在固定占据数空间基下执行普通偏转置以获得局域对数负性。

Result: 发现匹配的高阶Landau能级对产率满足纯电场形式的成对再分配关系，磁场仅通过能级产生概率调制；当粒子组占据不同物理Landau能级时，粒子-反粒子交叉关联出现不对称性，并导出解析刻画关系；获得局域对数负性的显式表达；Sauter型电场结果揭示关联对场参数与Landau结构的敏感依赖。

Conclusion: Landau能级的物理结构是调控Schwinger对纠缠性质的关键自由度；磁场不改变高能级间关联的拓扑形式，但通过能级简并破缺引入新的不对称性；该框架为强场QED中真空信息动力学提供了可计算的基准模型。

Abstract: We investigate mutual information and logarithmic negativity in fermionic Schwinger pair production in parallel electric and magnetic fields. Using spin-dependent Bogoliubov transformations, we construct the in-vacuum and derive the particle--antiparticle correlations for selected Landau modes. We also consider an initially entangled two-particle state and evaluate the correlations for six bipartitions. The physical Landau-level structure is treated explicitly by distinguishing modes with the same oscillator index from the spin representatives of a common physical Landau level. For matched higher levels, the pairwise redistribution relations retain the electric-only form, with the magnetic-field dependence entering through the Landau-level pair-production probabilities. When the two particle groups occupy different physical levels, the cross particle--antiparticle correlations become unequal, and we derive analytic relations characterizing this asymmetry. We further obtain the local logarithmic negativity from the ordinary partial transpose in a fixed occupation-space basis. Finally, we extend the analysis to a Sauter-type electric field in a constant magnetic background and examine the dependence of the pairwise correlations on the field parameters and Landau-level structure.

</details>


### [102] [From Integrability to Chaos in Tensionless AdS3 String Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25576)
*Matthias R. Gaberdiel,Beat Nairz,Cheng Peng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了对称积轨道流形 Sym^N(T^4) 的平面可积结构在量子引力修正下的行为，该模型对应于 AdS₃×S³×T⁴ 上的无张力弦理论；通过引入 R-R 通量变形并分析能级间距统计，发现主导非平面修正解除平面简并，并呈现随机矩阵行为，为弦相互作用诱导量子混沌提供了可控范例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子引力如何破坏可积性并诱发量子混沌，特别是在 AdS₃/CFT₂ 对应中由对称积 orbifold 描述的精确可解背景下。

Method: 基于 Sym^N(T^4) CFT 与 AdS₃×S³×T⁴ 上无张力弦的对偶性，引入 R-R 通量作为 bulk 变形参数，计算非平面（1/N）修正对能谱的影响，并通过能级间距分布（如 r-统计或 NNSD）诊断量子混沌特征。

Result: 主导非平面修正解除原平面极限下的大量能级简并，能级间距统计偏离泊松分布、趋近 GOE/GUE 随机矩阵预测，表明出现量子混沌特征。

Conclusion: 弦论中的非平面效应（即量子引力修正）可在严格可控的 AdS₃/CFT₂ 设置中驱动可积系统向量子混沌过渡，支持‘量子混沌是量子引力普适特征’的猜想。

Abstract: We study quantum-gravitational corrections to the planar integrable structure of the symmetric product orbifold $\text{Sym}^N(\mathbb{T}^4)$ that is dual to tensionless string theory on AdS$_3\times$S$^3\times \mathbb{T}^4$. We consider the deformation that switches on R-R flux in the bulk, and analyze the associated level spacing statistics. The leading non-planar corrections lift the planar degeneracies and produce signs of random-matrix behavior. Our results provide a controlled example of string interactions driving the onset of quantum chaos.

</details>


### [103] [A full-color four-point form factor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25591)
*Song He,Xuhang Jiang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了N=4超杨-米尔斯理论中全色两圈四点极大螺旋度破坏应力张量形状因子的符号和完整函数，涵盖N^1双迹与N^0单迹项；通过数值幺正性与主积分自举确定结果，形式因子自举直接复现其符号；置换外腿得到79字母表中的全部字母，N^0项含四个新非经典贡献；并导出完整的N^0两圈三共线硬分裂函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算高阶量子修正对检验AdS/CFT对应、理解散射振幅结构及共线极限行为具有关键意义，尤其在N=4 SYM这一可解模型中，应力张量形状因子是关联算符谱与散射动力学的核心可观测量。

Method: 结合数值幺正性（numerical unitarity）与主积分自举（master-integral bootstrap）固定积分系数；采用形式因子自举（form-factor bootstrap）独立重构符号；利用外腿置换对称性分析88字母表的约化；通过三共线极限提取硬分裂函数。

Result: 获得全色两圈四点MHV应力张量形状因子的完整解析表达（含符号与超越函数），确认N^0单迹部分含四个新非经典（即非多重对数型）超越结构；导出N^0两圈三共线硬分裂函数的完整形式；字母表由88缩减至79，且所有字母均可由平面字母表经外腿置换生成。

Conclusion: 该工作首次给出全色两圈应力张量形状因子的完整超越函数结构，揭示了非经典超越性在单迹扇区的新出现，验证了字母表的置换闭合性，并为QCD类理论中多尺度共线分裂函数的构造提供了严格模板。

Abstract: We determine the symbol and complete functions of the full-color two-loop four-point maximally helicity violating stress-tensor form factor in $\mathcal N=4$ super Yang-Mills theory, including $N^1$ double traces and $N^0$ single traces. Numerical unitarity and a master-integral bootstrap fix the result. A direct form-factor bootstrap reproduces the symbol. Permuting external legs in the planar 88-letter alphabet yields all 79 letters, while the $N^0$ terms contain four new nonclassical contributions. We also obtain the complete $N^0$ two-loop triple-collinear hard splitting functions.

</details>


### [104] [Two Faces of Spacetime Density Matrices: Entanglement and Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25634)
*Wu-zhong Guo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立了纠缠与复杂度之间通过时空密度矩阵的直接联系，提出了Krylov-Rényi复杂度和Krylov-von Neumann复杂度，二者均由时空副本矩与副本缺陷淬火的返回振幅定义，无需人为选取探针算符，具有非微扰定义和普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立纠缠与量子复杂度之间的基本联系，尤其在时序（timelike）背景下，超越空间子系统划分的传统框架，统一处理时空几何、动力学与信息测度。

Method: 基于时空密度矩阵与副本技巧（spacetime replica moments），将时序纠缠熵关联到 replica-defect quench 的返回振幅；由此定义 Krylov-Rényi 复杂度（以缺陷激发为种子，在复制哈密顿量下演化）；再由一阶副本数据取探针极限构造 Krylov-von Neumann 复杂度。

Result: Krylov-Rényi 与 Krylov-von Neumann 复杂度被明确定义，其种子态与演化由背景纯态、子系统时空几何及紫外截断自然确定；时序纠缠熵的全时间依赖完全决定 Krylov 动力学，从而编码复杂度增长；该复杂度是非微扰、普适且含动力学信息的。

Conclusion: 纠缠（特别是 timelike entanglement）与复杂度并非独立概念，而是同一时空信息结构的互补表征；Krylov-Rényi 复杂度提供了一个几何化、背景依赖但无需额外探针的复杂度定义，为 AdS/CFT 中复杂度=体积/作用量猜想提供了新的微观基础。

Abstract: We establish a direct connection between entanglement and complexity through spacetime density matrices. For stationary pure states and complementary regions at different times, spacetime replica moments encode the return amplitude of a replica-defect quench. This representation defines an associated state spread complexity, which we call Krylov-Rényi complexity. It can equivalently be obtained by using a replica-defect excitation of the replicated background state as the seed and evolving it under the original replicated Hamiltonian. We further construct Krylov-von Neumann complexity as a probe limit from first-order replica data. Timelike entanglement and complexity provide complementary information at a fixed time, while the full time dependence of the timelike entropy determines the Krylov dynamics and hence the complexity. The replica construction fixes the seed and its evolution through the background state, subsystem geometry, and regulator, without an independently chosen probe operator. Like entanglement entropy, the resulting complexity is defined nonperturbatively and probes universal properties and dynamical information about the underlying theory.

</details>


### [105] [Second-order formulation of chiral fermions with a local boundary condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25749)
*Kazuya Yonekura*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于二阶微分算符的等价连续形式，用于描述d维手征费米子，并给出了其自然的格点离散化方案，该方案自动包含Wilson项、避免费米子加倍，并与Shamir边界费米子构造密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服一阶Dirac算符加局域边界条件在路径积分定义中的技术困难，尤其在格点实现中；同时为无反常情形下纯d维手征费米子提供严格、可计算的构造。

Method: 采用d+1维大质量费米子在边界上的零模来实现d维手征性；将原本基于一阶Dirac算符的边界问题重表述为等价的二阶椭圆型算符本征值问题；在此基础上构造其保对称性的格点离散化，自然导出Wilson项。

Result: 建立了与原始一阶边界构造完全等价的二阶算符连续公式；其格点离散化自动满足Ginsparg-Wilson关系的某种变形，无谱退化，且不引入额外的手征模；解释了Shamir模型中边界费米子与Wilson参数之间的几何/算符起源。

Conclusion: 二阶算符框架不仅简化了手征费米子的路径积分定义，还为理解Wilson参数的物理本质（如其作为d+1维质量剖面的离散梯度）提供了新视角；该构造在无反常时给出良定义的、非微扰可处理的格点手征理论。

Abstract: Chiral fermions in $d$ dimensions arise naturally as boundary modes of a massive fermion in $d+1$ dimensions subject to a local boundary condition. When anomalies are absent, this construction can be used to define an intrinsically $d$-dimensional theory of chiral fermions. However, defining the path integral using the first-order Dirac operator with this boundary condition is technically inconvenient, even in the continuum. We present a formulation in terms of second-order differential operators that is completely equivalent to the original formulation in the continuum. Its natural lattice discretization generates Wilson's term and avoids fermion doubling. Our lattice formulation closely resembles the boundary-fermion construction of Shamir, and provides some insight into the case of a general Wilson parameter.

</details>


### [106] [Holographic Tessellations Tensor Networks $\{p,q\}$ from Entangled Quantum Tetrahedron in Loop Quantum Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25993)
*Deden M. Akbar,Freddy P. Zen,Jusak S. Kosasih*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一种基于LQG四面体间量子纠缠的全息张量网络，将自旋网络态与体-边对偶联系起来；通过等距嵌入得到类完美张量，并在有限网络上实现离散化的量子Ryu-Takayanagi公式；进一步应用于黑洞内部建模与Page曲线分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立圈量子引力（LQG）中自旋网络态与全息原理（特别是体-边对偶）之间的构造性联系，澄清几何自由度、面积算符、量子极值曲面及黑洞蒸发等概念在离散量子引力框架下的实现条件。

Method: 基于LQG中四面体间的SU(2)纠缠结构，构造超双曲{p,q}镶嵌上的全息张量网络；利用满秩微观转移映射、右逆与核空间补全技术实现等距嵌入；引入径向定向条件与适当归一化保证体-边映射的等距性；结合后选择非等距编码与Haar随机性假设分析信息保持与熵分支竞争。

Result: ① 对{5,4}镶嵌，六四面体构型生成具五条网络腿与一个逻辑指标的有效张量，且在满秩条件下可构造正比于完美张量的等距嵌入；② 推广至p=2n−1族镶嵌，局域维数支持绝对最大纠缠态（AME）；③ 在满足径向定向的有限网络上获得等距体-边映射；④ 边界熵分解为链贡献∑log(2j_ℓ+1)与体熵，实现离散量子RT结构；⑤ 通过后选择编码建模黑洞内部，结合Haar统计重现Page曲线的熵分支竞争。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了LQG几何自由度与全息码之间的首个构造性桥梁，明确了将LQG面积解释为边界熵的几何条件、量子极值曲面的定位机制，以及黑洞蒸发过程中信息恢复的动力学基础。

Abstract: The relationship between spin network states in loop quantum gravity and bulk--boundary duality is further explored using the tessellated holographic tensor network. We develop a holographic tensor network construction based on quantum entanglement between tetrahedra in loop quantum gravity (LQG). This construction relates four-valent intertwiner spaces and Bell states dressed with $SU(2)$ holonomies to effective tensors on hyperbolic $\{p,q\}$ tessellations. For the $\{5,4\}$ tessellation, six tetrahedra are assembled into a single effective cell with five network legs and one logical index. Provided that the microscopic transfer map has full row rank, a right inverse and a completion on the kernel space are employed to construct an isometric embedding, yielding an effective tensor proportional to a perfect tensor. The construction is generalized to the $p=2n-1$ family, with local dimensions supporting absolutely maximally entangled states. On finite networks satisfying a radial orientation condition, an appropriate normalization produces an isometric bulk--boundary map. For cuts satisfying the isometry condition on both sides, the boundary entropy decomposes into a link contribution $\sum_{\ell}\log(2j_{\ell}+1)$ and the bulk entropy, thereby providing a discrete realization of the quantum Ryu--Takayanagi structure. Applications to the black hole interior are formulated via postselection-based non-isometric encoding. Assuming additional Haar statistics, this framework is used to analyze the preservation of overlaps on average and the competition between entropy branches underlying the Page Curve. These results establish a constructive link between the geometric degrees of freedom of LQG and holographic codes, while clarifying the conditions required to connect them to geometric area in LQG, the location of the quantum extremal surface, and evaporation of black hole.

</details>


### [107] [Worldsheet Effective Theory of Abelian-Higgs Cosmic Strings beyond Nambu--Goto](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26013)
*Sergio Alameda-Calvo,Josu C. Aurrekoetxea,Jose J. Blanco-Pillado,Alberto García Martín-Caro,J.M. Queiruga*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从Abelian-Higgs模型出发，推导出宇宙弦世界面理论的协变形式，包含一个质量标量形变模与一个质量矢量模；通过对偶变换揭示其几何耦合结构（标量模耦合于内禀曲率，赝标量模耦合于法丛曲率），并解释格点模拟中观测到的参数不稳定性及碰撞波偏振依赖的内部模式激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越Nambu-Goto近似，建立能描述宇宙弦核心激发与横向Goldstone模能量交换的微观自洽世界面有效理论。

Method: 从临界耦合Abelian-Higgs模型出发，通过低能有效约化与协变世界面嵌入，导出含内禀自由度的世界面作用量；结合格点场论数值验证，并对矢量模作对偶变换以揭示几何耦合结构。

Result: 获得含质量标量（shape mode）和质量矢量（dualized to pseudoscalar）的世界面作用量；明确标量模耦合于世界面内禀曲率R，赝标量模耦合于法丛曲率F_{ab}；预言横向波偏振态决定两内部模式的选择性激发。

Conclusion: 宇宙弦的微观结构可通过协变世界面理论系统编码为曲率驱动的几何相互作用，为非线性弦动力学（如不稳定性、辐射、碰撞）提供可计算、可检验的超越Nambu-Goto框架。

Abstract: Cosmic strings can support long-lived excitations confined to their cores, opening channels for energy exchange with the transverse Goldstone modes that describe the string's motion. We derive a covariant worldsheet theory of their coupled dynamics directly from the Abelian-Higgs model at critical coupling, with all interaction coefficients fixed by the microscopic theory. The resulting action, which we validate against numerical lattice field theory simulations, extends the Nambu--Goto description to include a massive scalar shape mode and a massive vector mode. Dualizing the vector reveals a geometric distinction: the shape scalar mode couples to intrinsic worldsheet curvature, while the pseudoscalar couples to normal bundle curvature. These interactions explain the distinct parametric instabilities observed in lattice simulations and predict how the relative polarization of colliding transverse waves controls the excitation of the two internal modes. Our results connect microscopic string structure to the nonlinear worldsheet dynamics, providing a predictive framework for cosmic strings beyond the Nambu--Goto approximation.

</details>


### [108] [Equilibrium relativistic spin magnetohydrodynamics from chiral kinetic theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26087)
*Gopal Mistry,Amaresh Jaiswal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于手性动能理论的一阶相对论平衡自旋磁流体力学框架，适用于无质量手性费米子系统；通过协变Wigner函数导出磁修正壳层上的纯输运方程，确立了H定理与无散度熵流，并推导出任意自旋参考系下的平衡分布、守恒流、能量-动量张量、自旋张量、自旋极化及二阶磁涡旋响应，包括相对论Barnett磁化、反常电荷密度与转动诱导密度修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立微观自洽、热力学一致的相对论平衡自旋磁流体力学框架，弥合手性动能理论、自旋极化、磁化与反常输运之间的理论鸿沟。

Method: 从协变Wigner函数出发，将手性动能方程重写为磁位移质量壳上的纯流形方程，引入磁矩能与Berry曲率相空间测度；由此导出H定理与熵流；在任意自旋参考系下构造平衡分布并计算各守恒流与张量；结合热力学一致性约束确定二阶磁涡旋输运系数。

Result: 获得热力学自洽的自旋磁流体平衡态描述：包括守恒的熵流、frame-independent自旋极化、粒子/反粒子磁分裂、相对论Barnett磁化、温度与化学势无关的反常电荷密度、以及与场论精确结果一致的转动诱导密度修正。

Conclusion: 该框架为相对论自旋磁流体力学提供了统一微观基础，并建立了量子动能理论、自旋极化、磁化与反常输运之间的直接联系。

Abstract: We develop a microscopic and thermodynamically consistent framework for relativistic equilibrium spin magnetohydrodynamics of massless chiral fermions, directly from first-order chiral kinetic theory. Starting from the covariant Wigner function, we recast the chiral kinetic equation as a pure streaming equation on a magnetically shifted mass shell, incorporating both the magnetic-moment energy and the Berry-curvature phase-space measure. This formulation yields a kinetic $H$-theorem and an entropy current with vanishing divergence in global equilibrium. We construct the equilibrium distribution for an arbitrary spin frame and derive the associated currents, energy--momentum tensor, spin tensor, entropy current, and frame-independent spin polarization, including the magnetic splitting between particles and antiparticles. Thermodynamic consistency further determines the second-order magnetovortical responses, revealing a relativistic Barnett magnetization, a temperature- and chemical-potential-independent anomalous charge density, and rotation-induced density corrections that reproduce exact field-theory results. Our framework provides a unified microscopic foundation for relativistic equilibrium spin magnetohydrodynamics and establishes a direct link between quantum kinetic theory, spin polarization, magnetization, and anomalous transport.

</details>


### [109] [Cardinal RG=GR: Phase-Space Cardinality and the Cosmological Constant and Hierarchy Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26170)
*Laurent Freidel,Jerzy Kowalski-Glikman,Robert G. Leigh,Djordje Minic*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于相空间有限基数 N 的新有效场论框架，替代传统紫外截断，将时空与动量尺度统一关联；在Euclidean de Sitter空间中导出'Cardinal RG = GR'关系，由此自然给出与Gibbons-Hawking熵一致的N，并推得宇宙学常数正比于1/√N、具有辐射稳定性，同时解决等级问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准有效场论中由独立设定紫外截断引发的宇宙学常数问题和等级问题，规避对人为时空/动量尺度的依赖。

Method: 采用相空间有限维数（以无量纲基数 N 表征）作为基本量子限制；在Euclidean de Sitter背景下，将引力作用量的驻值条件重写为对动量尺度的驻值条件（即Cardinal RG=GR）；结合热力学与全息原理关联 N 与Gibbons-Hawking熵；运用该框架分析宇宙学常数及标量场质量参数的辐射修正。

Result: 1) N 严格等于 de Sitter 空间的 Gibbons-Hawking 熵；2) 宇宙学常数 Λ ∝ 1/√N，且一阶辐射修正被相空间截断自动压制；3) 标量场质量参数亦获类似辐射稳定性，等级问题得以缓解；4) 在 N → ∞ 且满足特定引力数据极限下，恢复标准QFT与重整化群结果。

Conclusion: 相空间基数 N 是比独立紫外截断更基本的物理参数；Cardinal RG 提供一个自洽、全息兼容、天然解决自然性问题的有效场论新范式。

Abstract: The effective field theory approach constructs the low-energy theory by integrating out fluctuations above an ultraviolet scale which raises the cosmological constant and hierarchy problems. We investigate an alternative prescription in which the quantum theory is restricted to a finite accessible region of phase space, characterized by a dimensionless cardinality $\mathcal N$, rather than by independently prescribed spacetime and momentum scales. At fixed cardinality these scales are related. For Euclidean de Sitter space, the stationarity condition for the gravitational action can then be written as a stationarity condition with respect to the momentum scale, a relation which we call Cardinal RG=GR. We demonstrate the relation between $\mathcal N$ and the Gibbons-Hawking entropy of de Sitter space, compute the cosmological constant proportional to ${1}/{\sqrt{\mathcal N}}$, show that it is radiatively stable and apply the same prescription to demonstrate the radiative stability of the mass parameter for a simple interacting scalar field model, thereby illuminating the resolution of the hierarchy problem. We also establish a precise limit, involving gravitational data, in which the conventional results of Quantum Field Theory and the Renormalization Group are recovered.

</details>


### [110] [Full-Color Two-loop Four-point Form Factor in N=4 SYM with Global CK-duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26194)
*Zhiming Cai,Yuanhong Guo,Gang Yang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了N=4超杨-米尔斯理论中应力能张量超多重态的全色双圈四点MHV形状因子，利用色-动量对偶与幺正割线构造了显式满足全局离壳色-动量对偶的完整一、二圈被积函数；经积分分部约化至均匀超越度主积分后，结果呈现为有理数乘以简单运动学因子的形式，体现最大超越性；进一步用五边形函数表示该形状因子，验证了全色红外极点相消，提取了有限部分，并首次给出了双圈三共线分裂函数与双软流的非平面结果。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 结合色-动量对偶构型（BCJ型）与广义幺正割线法构造离壳被积函数，采用积分分部（IBP）约化至均匀超越度主积分基，辅以五边形函数解析表示与红外极点结构分析。

Abstract: We determine the full-color two-loop four-point MHV form factor of the stress-tensor supermultiplet in N=4 super-Yang-Mills theory. Using color-kinematics (CK) duality and unitarity cuts, we construct complete one- and two-loop integrands manifesting global off-shell CK duality without explicit $\mu^2$ dependence. Integration-by-parts reduction to uniform-transcendentality master integrals yields purely rational numbers multiplying simple kinematic prefactors, manifesting maximal transcendentality. Expressing the form factor in terms of pentagon functions, we verify full-color infrared-pole cancellation, extract the finite remainder, and obtain new non-planar results for the two-loop triple-collinear splitting function and double-soft current.

</details>

Abstract: We present a unified analytic solution to the BPS equations of spherical Dp-branes for $1\leq p \leq 5$ and give the corresponding type II uplifts in closed form. These are holographically dual to maximally supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory on $S^{p+1}$. The change of variables that makes the BPS equations integrable also allows us to analytically calculate a universal result for the holographic Wilson loop and obtain UV asymptotic behaviour previously determined numerically. The same change of variables extends naturally, with suitable modifications, to the cases $p=0$ and $p=-1$. We obtain a 1-parameter family of D0-brane solutions and show how the IR conditions lead to a singular limit of the integration constant. We also solve the D(-1)-brane BPS equations and show the resulting Euclidean uplift fails regularity conditions. Separately, we obtain an explicit representation of the one-ball electrostatic solution of polarised IKKT, which lets us express multipole moments and holographic observables using elliptic invariants. Finally, we analytically solve the BPS equations of the holographic dual of $\mathcal{N}=2^\ast$ supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory on $S^4$, present the corresponding type IIB uplift and calculate the holographic Wilson loop.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于全息对偶，在五维Einstein-Maxwell-Chern-Simons引力背景中求解线性化流体/引力方程，严格保留频率ω与动量k的全依赖性，通过规范不变的膜世界方法提取各输运通道的记忆核与色散关系。

Result: 1) 获得广义剪切η(ω,k)、电导σ(ω,k)、第三阶梯度输运函数τ_π(ω,k)及手征涡旋系数ξ_V(ω,k)的完整频动量依赖形式；2) 发现ξ_V(ω,k)具有非零建立时间，其记忆核在ω→0时呈幂律衰减~ω^{−1/2}；3) 近极端极限下所有通道的记忆核出现共性长时尾部~t^{−3/2}。

Conclusion: 有限密度显著改写强耦合等离子体输运的时间层次结构，导数阶数不再是时间响应快慢的可靠指标；非局域性与记忆效应由黑洞视界附近的近极端几何所主导，体现为共性的长时间幂律弛豫。

Abstract: We investigate linearized all-order transport in a finite-density holographic plasma described by five-dimensional Einstein-Maxwell-Chern-Simons theory. By retaining the full frequency- and momentum-dependence of the fluid/gravity equations, we determine generalized shear, diffusion, higher-gradient, and chiral-vortical transport functions together with their real-time memory kernels. The different channels exhibit a clear hierarchy of temporal response: the leading shear and diffusion sectors respond promptly, whereas the higher-gradient sectors develop only after a finite buildup time. Remarkably, the chiral-vortical response also shows a finite buildup time despite entering at first order in the hydrodynamic derivative expansion, demonstrating that derivative order does not by itself determine the temporal organization of transport. Toward the near-extremal regime, long-time memory becomes increasingly persistent across several channels. These results reveal how finite density reshapes nonlocal transport and relaxation in strongly coupled holographic plasmas.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于Feynman图识别局部生成元，分析其满足的代数关系；对SU(2)-类子扇区构造Temperley-Lieb代数；在N=2 quiver理论中区分两种SU(2)-类子扇区，分别推导其动力学TL代数；引入色依赖Drinfeld扭刻画新型代数与XXZ TL代数的关系。

Result: 1) Feynman图自然编码TL代数结构；2) N=4 SYM中恢复标准TL代数；3) N=2 quiver理论中获得两类动力学TL代数：一类具色依赖loop fugacity，另一类使三次TL关系动力学化；4) 后者可由局域、非同态、色依赖的Drinfeld扭从XXZ TL代数导出；5) TL结构统一处理谱问题并揭示不可约表示结构；6) 初步构建对应loop模型。

Conclusion: Temperley-Lieb代数是单圈尺度算符的核心组织原理，其动力学推广深刻反映了N=2超共形quiver理论的几何（quiver节点着色）与场论（bifundamental/adjoint标量组合）结构；图示语言与代数扭变换为解析谱与表示论提供了新范式。

Abstract: The one-loop dilatation operator of a superconformal planar gauge theory can be written as a sum of local generators. The algebra these generators satisfy is a powerful organising principle for the spectrum. For SU(2)-like bosonic subsectors we show that this algebra can be read off directly from Feynman diagrams, which we identify as a natural graphical language for it. For N=4 super Yang-Mills this simply reproduces the Temperley-Lieb (TL) algebra of the XXX spin chain. After orbifolding and marginally deforming to N=2 superconformal quiver theories, the same recipe yields dynamical generalisations of the TL algebra. In these theories, the faces of the diagrams are coloured according to the quiver nodes, and separated by domain walls corresponding to bifundamental fields. There are two inequivalent SU(2)-like subsectors, which lead to two different TL algebras. One sector is built from the two bifundamental scalars and (in the simplest case) gives a dynamical TL algebra with a colour-dependent loop fugacity in its quadratic relation. The other sector is built from one bifundamental and one adjoint scalar, and (always) gives a novel algebra, in which the cubic TL relation becomes dynamical. In this latter case, we show that each generator of this new algebra is related to a standard XXZ TL generator by a colour-dependent twist. This twist is local and not an algebra homomorphism, and can be written as a dynamical Drinfeld twist. In both sectors, the TL structure provides a natural graphical approach to the spectral problem, as well as clarifying the structure of the irreducible representations of these chains. Finally, we give a preliminary description of the loop models associated to these algebras.

</details>

*Jay Armas,Giorgos Batzios,Pedro Vicente Marto,Chiara Toldo*

Method: 结合blackfold有效理论与广义对称性（特别是2-群对称性）框架，从带探针源的超引力出发推导2-群（非）守恒律，构建二维2-群流体模型，通过热力学匹配确定有效理论参数，并采用微扰方法构造多参数非极端与极端黑洞解。

Conclusion: 广义对称性（尤其是2-群结构）为系统组织高维黑洞相图提供了自然框架；blackfold方法经此拓展后可统一描述非极端与极端、平直与AdS背景下的复杂黑洞构型，揭示了对称性不变量在有效理论构造中的核心作用。

Abstract: We investigate the black hole phase diagram of five-dimensional minimal supergravity using the blackfold approach and the framework of generalized symmetries. Starting from supergravity coupled to probe sources, we derive the 2-group (non-)conservation laws, construct the equilibrium action for a two-dimensional 2-group fluid, and compute the associated global conserved charges. We then match the thermodynamics of the general non-extremal black string in the ungauged theory to that of this fluid, organizing the blackfold effective theory and its solutions in terms of symmetry invariants. In the non-extremal ungauged sector, we perturbatively construct new black rings, Saturns, di-rings, and bi-rings, including a black Saturn with seven independent parameters. Taking appropriate extremal limits, we identify several extremal branches, some previously unexplored and others anticipated, as well as BPS branches that approximate known supersymmetric families. Finally, we apply the same framework to construct extremal black rings in AdS$_5$, identifying three regular branches that are promising candidates for an exact completion.

</details>


### [115] [Classification of Chern-Simons Couplings and Deformed Pull-Backs on D-Branes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26374)
*Armin Hatefi,Ehsan Hatefi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个用于分类Dp-膜上高阶导数Ramond-Ramond（RR）耦合的新框架，基于RR态、规范场与两个横向标量的完整散射振幅；提出以混合Chern-Simons配边算子修正拉回操作，并提出三条关于有效耦合空间有限性、偶形式族存在性及接触项张量结构生成性的核心猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Myers和Taylor展开无法完备描述包含无限质量壳极点与高阶导数局部项的RR耦合；需从散射振幅第一性原理出发，系统刻画高导数RR相互作用的张量结构与等价类。

Method: 基于闭弦RR态、规范场与横向标量三体散射振幅的低能展开；引入高阶拉回算子P_∞与混合配边算子T_∞；结合振幅极点留数与局域接触项提取物理数据；运用等价关系（如运动方程、Bianchi恒等式、场重定义）对耦合空间进行模约化。

Result: 确立了三类核心猜想：(1) 固定场内容与导数阶数下，有效RR耦合构成有限维向量空间；(2) 存在一族由有限个偶形式参数化的RR–规范–标量混合相互作用；(3) 高导数接触项可由有限基本张量结构与导数符号多项式生成；并明确定义P_∞与T_∞作为形式化工具。
