Abstract: We establish a framework for classifying higher-derivative Ramond-Ramond (RR) couplings on Dp-branes, focusing on the complete scattering amplitude of a closed-string RR state, a gauge field, and two transverse scalars. The low-energy expansion reveals infinite massless poles and local interaction terms, surpassing standard Myers and Taylor expansions. We propose a higher-class deformation of the pullback using mixed Chern-Simons transgression operators to address these complexities. Our three conjectures are as follows: 1. For fixed field content and derivative order, effective couplings form a finite-dimensional space under stated equivalences. 2. A family of mixed RR, gauge, and scalar interactions exists at all orders, represented by a finite number of effective even forms. 3. The higher-derivative contact-term sector can be constructed from a finite set of basic tensor structures using polynomials in derivative symbols. We introduce a higher-class pullback operator \( P_{\infty} \) and mixed transgression operators \( \mathcal{T}_{\infty} \), emphasizing amplitude-derived pole residues and local contact terms as key physical data. This formalism distinguishes admissible tensor structures, presenting characteristic-form aspects as conjectures alongside their validity considerations.

</details>


### [116] [Inhomogeneous Cutoff Regularization of Loops in Cosmological Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26495)
*Jacopo Fumagalli,Keisuke Inomata*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了在宇宙学关联函数的圈图修正计算中使用截断正则化的方法，提出了一种基于局域背景几何、适用于超视界扰动的物理尺度截断方案，并证明其能自动消除紫外边界项、保证功率谱和重子谱的一致性关系及晚时守恒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统截断正则化在膨胀宇宙背景下未适配局域物理尺度，导致超视界扰动下圈图计算中出现不合理的紫外边界项，破坏功率谱晚时守恒与一致性关系；需构建与分离宇宙图像相容、保持残余对称性的正则化方案。

Method: 引入依赖局域弗里德曼-勒梅特-罗伯逊-沃尔克（FLRW）度规的非均匀截断Λ(x)，在分离宇宙图像下按物理波数k_phys = a(η,x) k_conformal实施；将Λ(x)在长波背景中展开，导出由大尺度模插入引起的调节器诱导项，并采用动量空间费曼规则结合残余对称性（空间标度不变性与体积守恒各向异性重标度）进行系统计算。

Result: 1) 一阶标量与张量功率谱的紫外边界项被截断诱导项精确抵消，确立其晚时守恒；2) 挤压极限下一阶标量三谱满足Maldacena一致性关系（至有限截断精度）；3) 一阶三谱本身亦具晚时守恒性；4) 截断方案保持残余空间标度对称性，适用于软外腿过程。

Conclusion: 局域物理截断是宇宙学圈图计算中自洽且对称性友好的正则化方法；其诱导的长模插入项具有清晰的物理图像与图解结构，是保障晚时渐近行为与软定理成立的关键机制。

Abstract: We revisit the use of cutoff regularization in computing loop corrections to cosmological correlators. We argue that a cutoff corresponding to a fixed physical scale must be defined with respect to the local background geometry. In the presence of superhorizon perturbations, this is naturally implemented within the separate universe picture, where different local patches are regulated at the same physical scale. Expanding this cutoff in the long-wavelength background generates a series of regulator-induced contributions to loop amplitudes, which admit a diagrammatic interpretation as long-mode insertions on the corresponding loop contractions. Our cutoff prescription preserves residual spatial dilatations and volume-preserving anisotropic rescalings. It is therefore particularly relevant for loop correlators with soft external modes. We illustrate the mechanism by computing the contributions to the one-loop scalar and tensor power spectra on superhorizon scales. In both cases, the additional corrections induced by the inhomogeneous cutoff cancel the ultraviolet boundary terms arising in the ordinary diagrams. These calculations establish, within cutoff regularization, the late-time constancy of both power spectra and clarify its physical origin. We then investigate the squeezed one-loop scalar bispectrum and show that the additional cutoff contributions are essential for satisfying the consistency relation at finite cutoff. Finally, we discuss the role of the inhomogeneous cutoff in the late-time constancy of the one-loop bispectrum.

</details>


### [117] [Twisting Spinning Particles with Superghost Backgrounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26554)
*Erik Fink,Jingqian Gou,Niklas Holbach,Ondrej Hulik*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在经典BV形式论框架下，通过赋予世界线局部超对称性的玻色幽灵以常数背景，构造了自旋粒子的扭转（twist）；该方法类比Costello-Li对超引力的扭转；通过多项式态表示超幽灵，并绝对/相对地施加平移与未平移的R-对称性约束；在平坦靶空间上，N=2、N=4与N=8粒子分别导出de Rham、Dolbeault与多项式线性BCOV复形，后两者对应开/闭B模型线性化场内容；配对与相互作用需额外靶空间结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立世界线描述中自旋粒子的拓扑扭转，使之与靶空间拓扑场论（如B模型）的线性化场复形相匹配，并统一理解Costello-Li型超引力扭转的世界线类比。

Method: 采用经典BV形式论；引入常数背景至世界线局部N-扩展超对称的玻色幽灵（即世界线超重力幽灵）；在多项式态上实现超幽灵代数；选择性地对世界线R-对称性进行规范固定；将R-电荷约束分为‘绝对施加’（shifted）与‘相对施加’（unshifted）两类以实现扭转。

Result: 在平坦靶流形上：N=2粒子 → de Rham复形；N=4粒子（复靶空间）→ Dolbeault复形；N=8粒子（含两味费米子）→ 多项式线性BCOV复形；后两者精确重现开B模型（Chiral de Rham）与闭B模型（BCOV）的线性化量子场内容；复形微分由约化世界线微分算子生成。

Conclusion: 世界线BV扭转提供了一种系统性机制，将局部超对称粒子模型映射至靶空间拓扑场论的线性化复形；该框架揭示了世界线超对称性破缺模式与靶空间复几何/拓扑结构之间的精确对应；但完整相互作用需引入非平凡靶空间微分几何结构（如Kähler度量、Calabi-Yau结构等）。

Abstract: We construct twists of spinning particles in the classical BV formalism by giving constant backgrounds to the bosonic ghosts of local worldline supersymmetry, in analogy with Costello and Li's twist of supergravity, which gives a nonzero background to the ghost of local spacetime supersymmetry. We represent the superghosts on polynomial states and gauge selected worldline R symmetries, imposing the shifted R constraints absolutely and the unshifted ones relatively. On flat targets, the N=2 particle, its N=4 extension on a complex target and an N=8 particle with two fermion flavors give the de Rham, Dolbeault and polynomial linear BCOV complexes. The last two are the linearized field content of the open and closed B-model target-space theories. Pairings and interactions require target-space input beyond the reduced worldline differentials.

</details>


### [118] [UV Bounds on Inflationary Parity Violation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26594)
*Alexander Cassem,Mark P. Hertzberg*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了宇宙大尺度上宇称破缺的可能性，特别是通过暴胀期间动力学Chern-Simons算符在UV完备模型（如费米子与弦论模型）中生成可观测奇宇称四点关联函数（trispectrum）的可行性；结果表明现有TSS三谱（bispectrum）观测限制已严重压制该信号，除非张标比极小且TSS顶点被极端放大，否则与有效理论自洽性冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验宇宙大尺度结构中是否存在可观测的宇称破缺，受四点物质关联函数分析及动力学Chern-Simons修正引力理论的启发。

Method: 在具体UV完备框架（含费米子耦合与弦论实现）下，结合当前TSS bispectrum观测约束，分析动力学Chern-Simons项对奇宇称trispectrum的贡献及其理论自洽性边界。

Result: 现有TSS bispectrum上限强烈压制奇宇称trispectrum振幅；当r接近当前观测极限时，可观测信号与UV完成的有效性范围冲突；仅当r极小且TSS顶点被极大增强时才可能保留可探测性。

Conclusion: 在自洽有效场论框架内生成可观测大尺度宇称破缺信号极为困难；未来搜寻仍具科学价值，但理论实现需突破标准EFT范式。

Abstract: A question of fundamental interest is whether parity is violated on large scales in the Universe. This possibility has been motivated by recent analyses of the four-point matter correlation function. It has also been suggested that detectable parity violation could be generated during inflation by the dynamical Chern-Simons operator, a leading effective field theory correction to general relativity. In this work, we examine this possibility in realistic UV completions, including fermionic and string-theoretic examples. We show that existing bounds on the tensor-scalar-scalar (TSS) bispectrum strongly constrain the possibility of generating a detectable parity-odd trispectrum. For tensor-to-scalar ratios near current observational limits, detectability is incompatible with, or pushes against, the regime of validity of these UV completions. A detectable signal can remain possible for a sufficiently small tensor-to-scalar ratio, but only at the cost of an extremely large enhancement of the TSS vertex. Thus, while a future search for large scale parity violation is worth pursuing, generating a detectable signal within a consistent effective theory is highly nontrivial.

</details>


### [119] [Equivariant iterated Eisenstein integrals for congruence subgroups of level 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26597)
*Emiel Claasen,Mehregan Doroudiani,Claude Duhr,Axel Kleinschmidt,Franca Lippert,Oliver Schlotterer,Yoann Sohnle*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了针对Γ(2)和Γ₀(2)的等变迭代Eisenstein积分，通过结合其复共轭与单值化2阶分圆多重zeta值，生成非全纯模形式；该构造基于生成级数（取值于pro-unipotent李群），并系统确定了控制zeta插入的2阶zeta生成元及其李括号关系；在权为2的Eisenstein级数情形下，恢复出模曲线Γ(2)\H上的单值多重对数函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全纯Eisenstein级数的模性质无法直接延拓至其原函数（即积分）；为构建具有良定义模变换行为的非全纯对象，需引入分圆多重zeta值作为非齐次项，并实现单值化。

Method: 采用Brown型生成级数方法，将迭代Eisenstein积分（针对Γ(2)与Γ₀(2)）、其复共轭及单值2阶分圆多重zeta值进行组合；引入zeta生成元对pro-nilpotent李代数进行正规化，并系统求解其李括号关系。

Result: 构造出Γ(2)与Γ₀(2)上的等变迭代Eisenstein积分，构成非全纯模形式；获得2阶zeta生成元的完整李代数结构；在权2情形下，导出Γ(2)\H上的单值多重对数函数。

Conclusion: 迭代Eisenstein积分的模不变性必须通过引入分圆多重zeta值实现单值化与等变化；该框架统一了SL₂(ℤ)情形下的Brown理论，并推广至2阶同余子群，揭示了模曲线上单值特殊函数与混合模Tate动机之间的深层联系。

Abstract: The modular properties of holomorphic Eisenstein series for $\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb{Z})$ and its congruence subgroups do not carry over to their primitives. Instead, the transformation of iterated Eisenstein integrals under congruence subgroups of level $N$ introduces inhomogeneous terms comprising (among others) cyclotomic multiple zeta values of level $N$. We combine iterated Eisenstein integrals for the congruence subgroups of level $N=2$ of arbitrary iteration depth with their complex conjugates and (single-valued) level-2 cyclotomic multiple zeta values such that they define non-holomorphic modular forms. Our construction of these so-called equivariant iterated Eisenstein integrals for $\Gamma(2)$ and $\Gamma_0(2)$ proceeds at the level of generating series that take values in an infinite-dimensional (pro-unipotent) Lie group and closely follow Brown's analogous construction in the $\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb{Z})$ case. The required insertions of cyclotomic multiple zeta values are controlled by zeta generators for level 2 that normalise the corresponding (pro-nilpotent) Lie algebra of the generating series and whose Lie-bracket relations we determine systematically. Upon restriction to Eisenstein series of modular weight~2, we recover single-valued multiple polylogarithms on the genus 0 modular curve $\Gamma(2)\backslash \mathbb{H}$.

</details>


### [120] [Entropy Products and the Extremal Limit That Never Comes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26628)
*Kevin Goldstein,Vishnu Jejjala,Suresh Nampuri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Einstein-Maxwell-标量理论中构造了具有两个正则黑洞视界的渐近平直解，发现其视界熵积不唯一由电荷和耦合常数决定；不同分支可共享相同电荷、耦合及渐近标量场值，但熵积可高于或低于极值熵的平方；熵积在质量与渐近标量场变化时保持守恒，且无真正极值极限（温度仅在质量发散处趋于零）；作者证明了该类解的局域存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验正则黑洞熵积是否普遍具有电荷与耦合依赖的普适性，并探究标量场对黑洞热力学结构的影响。

Method: 基于Einstein-Maxwell-标量引力理论，在渐近平直时空下构造含两个正则视界的黑洞解；通过分析标量场在视界处的正则性条件约束其取值；系统研究熵积、温度、质量与渐近标量场之间的函数关系；运用常微分方程初值问题理论证明局域解的存在性。

Result: 发现熵积不固定于电荷与耦合，而可在极值熵平方上下连续变化；不同解分支可具有完全相同的宏观参数（Q, g, φ∞），但熵积不同；熵积为守恒量，不随质量或φ∞变化；温度零点仅出现在质量发散处，故无光滑极值极限；所有解满足视界正则性条件。

Conclusion: 正则黑洞熵积不具备普适的电荷-耦合依赖性；标量场自由度导致热力学相空间出现非平凡分支结构；熵积守恒性暗示其可能对应某拓扑或几何不变量；该结果对黑洞微观统计诠释及AdS/CFT中的标量毛发问题具启示意义。

Abstract: Two regular black hole horizons need not have an entropy product fixed by the charges and couplings. In Einstein--Maxwell--scalar theory, we construct asymptotically flat branches with products above or below the squared extremal entropy. Regularity selects their horizon scalar values, and different branches can share the charges, couplings, and asymptotic scalar. Each product stays constant as mass and asymptotic scalar vary. The horizon areas stay apart and no extremal limit is reached: the temperature vanishes only where the mass diverges. We prove local existence.

</details>
